# SwiftUI API gap

**Goal:** list what SwiftUI of macOS 27.2.1 offers and BarrierKit lacks, rank the
gaps by how much they limit the design of apps, and say what each one needs.
**Scope:** the public interfaces of `SwiftUI` and `SwiftUICore` in the Xcode 27.2
SDK, against `bk`, `ctdesign` and `ctappearance` on `main`. It covers views,
modifiers, style protocols with their static members, environment values,
scenes, navigation, input, state, animation, graphics, text and accessibility.
It leaves out the frameworks around SwiftUI (Core Data, WidgetKit, Charts) and
the AppKit-only APIs; [`appkit-comparison.md`](appkit-comparison.md) and the
[coverage matrix](swiftui-appkit-coverage.md) hold the AppKit side. The
matrix is the one source of each API's current status; the tables below are
the review of the snapshot and rank the gaps.
**Snapshot:** `main` at `b52708df`, 2026-11-21; refreshed on 2026-11-22 ([top](#what-an-app-author-still-needs)).
**Reader:** the owner who picks the next work, and the engineer or agent who
closes a gap.
**Input:** the two `.swiftinterface` files, [the glossary](../barrierkit/20-glossary.md),
[the coverage matrix](swiftui-appkit-coverage.md), the code of `crates/`, the
sources of Freya `=0.5.3` and its Skia bindings in the cargo registry, and
[`decisions.md`](../decisions.md).
**Output:** a status for every item of the closed list, a table of gaps per
area with size and blocker, the macOS 26 and 27 news, and a top 20.

## What an app author still needs

Refresh of 2026-11-22 against the tree after the `State<bool>` sheets ([Sheet](../barrierkit/11-views.md#sheet)). Ranked by how often an
app written in SwiftUI or AppKit needs it, open items only. Each line has the
`grep` that backs it, run from the repository root.

1. **Rich text**: `Text + Text`, inline Markdown, `textSelection`. **Done for the three forms ([Text runs](../barrierkit/06-text-and-editing.md#text-runs)):** `grep -rnE 'impl.*Add.*for Text|fn markdown|fn text_selection' crates/bk/src` → `Text + Text`, `Text::markdown`, `rich_text::markdown`, `Text::text_selection`; `bold`, `italic`, `underline` and `strikethrough` on `Text`. **Still open:** `Text(Image)` and interpolation (`grep -rn 'Text(Image\|fn image' crates/bk/src/elements.rs` → 0), block Markdown (headings, lists), a link that opens inside a selectable text, colour and paragraph attributes in `TextEditor` ([rich-text plan](rich-text-plan.md)).
2. **`AnyLayout`, several proposals, measured `ViewThatFits`**. `ZStack`, `overlay`, `background_view`, `Layout` (placement only, `LayoutContainer`) and `ViewThatFits` (each candidate states its extent) exist ([Custom layout](../barrierkit/11-views.md#custom-layout), `crates/bk/src/layout/`); `grep -rnE 'AnyLayout|ViewSpacing|size_that_fits|LayoutValueKey' crates` → 0. Open: torin measures each subview once, so a layout cannot ask for another size, an overlay's `fill` child fills the container and not the base, and a parent that centers a container places it by its first subview.
3. **Accessibility modifiers by SwiftUI name** (mostly done, [Accessibility modifiers](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#accessibility-modifiers)). `grep -rn 'fn accessibility_\(label\|hint\|value\|hidden\|identifier\|element\|add_traits\)' crates/bk/src` → 7 (`accessibility.rs`), on every view and control; `grep -rn 'accesskit_macos' crates/bk/src` finds the macOS adapter, so VoiceOver and the AX API read the tree (probe: `axdump` shows `title="Add a task" help=… id=add-task`); `TextField`, `SearchField` and `TextEditor` expose name, value and placeholder. Still open: macOS maps the label to AXTitle, not AXDescription, and `Heading` is not `AXHeading` (`accesskit_macos`); no `AXSelectedTextRange` for fields; `accessibility_element` is not applied on Linux; no `accessibilitySortPriority`, `accessibilityInputLabels`, `accessibilityAction` or `accessibilityRotor`.
4. **`ConcentricRectangle`, arbitrary clips**. `Rectangle`, `RoundedRectangle`, `Capsule`, `Circle`, `Ellipse`, `Path` and `clipShape` for rounded shapes exist ([Shapes](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#shapes), `crates/bk/src/shapes.rs`); `grep -rn 'ConcentricRectangle' crates` → 0; an ellipse or a path cannot clip.
5. **`matchedGeometryEffect`, `numericText`, `slide`/`push`/`blurReplace`**. `Transition` (`OPACITY`, `SCALE`, `scale`, `offset`, `move_edge`, `combined`, `asymmetric`), `Presence` (keeps the leaving view mounted; runs inside `with_animation`) and `contentTransition(.opacity)` exist ([Transitions](../barrierkit/14-animations.md#transitions), `crates/bk/src/transition.rs`, `grep -n 'pub fn\|pub const' crates/bk/src/transition.rs`). Open: `grep -rniE 'matched_?geometry|numeric_?text|blur_replace' crates/bk/src` → 0 (a shared element needs a frame registry between two places of the tree and a first frame at the old place, before layout; `numericText` is per glyph); a removal outside a `Presence` has no transition.
6. **Typed drag and drop and clipboard**: images, rich text, `Transferable`. **Done for the clipboard and for dragging out on macOS ([Typed clipboard](../barrierkit/13-app.md#typed-clipboard)):** `DragPayload::{Files, Text, Html, Image}` (`drag.rs`), `pasteboard::{Transferable, Pasteboard}` over `NSPasteboard` (`platform/macos/pasteboard.rs`), and an `NSDraggingSession` from the content view (`grep -rn 'beginDraggingSession' crates/bk/src` → `macos/view.rs`). **Still open:** a drop target for typed data (`grep -rniE 'registerForDraggedTypes|on_drop' crates/bk/src` → 0; only files arrive on Linux, nothing on macOS), images and files on the Linux clipboard (`platform/linux/pasteboard.rs` is text only), a typed `Transferable` for app structs beyond the four payloads, `copyable`/`pasteDestination` modifiers.
7. **`keyboardShortcut` on controls other than `Button`**. `.keyboard_shortcut` reaches every `Button` below it ([Modifiers on controls](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#modifiers-on-controls)); `Toggle`, `Link` and the like do not read it.
8. **`scrollTargetBehavior`, `defaultScrollAnchor`**. `scrollPosition`, `ScrollPosition`, `onScrollGeometryChange` and `onScrollPhaseChange` exist ([Scroll position](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#scroll-position), `crates/bk/src/scroll/position.rs`); `grep -rn 'scroll_target_behavior' crates` → 0.
9. **List behavior**: `swipeActions` (touch-first, not built, [List row modifiers](../barrierkit/11-views.md#list-row-modifiers)) and selection by id. `grep -rniE 'swipe' crates/bk/src` → 0.
10. **`Glass.tint`, `.interactive()`, `glassEffectUnion`, and the subtree effect modifiers**. `.glass_effect(Glass::REGULAR | CLEAR, Shape)` works on any view and its subtree on macOS 26 and later through the native glass path, with the kit's blur or opaque solid below ([Glass on any view](../barrierkit/15-backdrops-and-media.md#glass-on-any-view), `StyleModifiers::glass_effect`, `GlassContainer` of one item). Open: `grep -rniE 'glass_effect_union|fn tint|fn interactive' crates/bk/src/glass_container.rs crates/bk/src/modifiers.rs` → 0 (a tint is a new wire material); on macOS a nested glass of another radius or variant is its own native view, while the wire of Malaysia still holds one of each, and a window draws 8 glasses; the look matches SwiftUI to the pixel in a key or non-key window, light and dark, except an uneven shape (one radius) and Reduce Transparency and Increase Contrast (a flat fill, [comparison](glass-effect-comparison.md)); `blur`, `blendMode`, `mask` exist only as `CompositingGroup` effects (ADR 0103), not as modifiers.
11. **Document apps** (smallest macOS version done, [Documents and file dialogs](../barrierkit/08-windows.md#documents-and-file-dialogs) supersedes ADR 0085 for documents). `grep -rnE 'windowShouldClose|openURLs|applicationShouldTerminate' crates/bk/src` → `macos/view.rs` and `macos/delegate.rs`: an edited window (`use_document`) shows the dot, is vetoed on the native close, shows the "Save changes?" `NSAlert` sheet and saves (Cmd-W and Cmd-Q were not run); `on_open_url` receives Finder and `open` files. Still open: `grep -rniE 'FileDocument|DocumentGroup|NSDocument' crates/bk/src` → 0 (no document type, autosave, versions or recent-documents menu), the kit's own traffic lights and `WindowControls::close()` close without asking, a quit that walks every edited window, an `Info.plist` generator, Linux.
12. **Dialogs attached to their window, with filters** (done on macOS, [Documents and file dialogs](../barrierkit/08-windows.md#documents-and-file-dialogs)). `FileDialog`, `.file_importer`, `.file_exporter` and `.file_dialog_default_directory` open `NSOpenPanel` and `NSSavePanel` as a sheet of the window with extensions and a start folder (`macos/panels.rs`). Still open: filters are extensions, not `UTType` (`grep -rn 'UTType' crates/bk/src` finds only a comment); on Linux `grep -rn 'xdg.foreign' crates` → 0 and `portal.rs` takes no parent, filters or start folder.
13. **Right-to-left in `List`, `Table`, the sidebar and the title bar**. `.layout_direction(..)` mirrors `HStack`, `VStack`, `Frame`, `Label`, `Text`, `Grid`, `ZStack`, custom layouts and `Form`, and sets the six control styles ([Right to left](../barrierkit/11-views.md#right-to-left), `crates/bk/src/layout/direction.rs`). Open: `grep -rniE 'layout_?direction_?behavior|flips_for' crates` → 0; no direction from the locale; padding is physical; Freya never sets the paragraph direction.

Done since the snapshot (each has its evidence in the [Top 20](#top-20-gaps)
and in the rows marked "(was …)"): views in list rows and table cells, `Label`,
`Spacer`, `Grid`, `Frame`, `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `Settings`, `openWindow`,
`Commands`, `NavigationStack`, `NavigationLink`, ambient `disabled`,
environment values, `FocusState`, the gesture layer, implicit animation,
scroll physics, and `sheet`, `alert` and `popover` driven by a `State<bool>`
([Sheet](../barrierkit/11-views.md#sheet)). `onHover` and `onTapGesture(count:)` joined them in the same change.

**How the refresh ran.** A script re-ran the 171 `grep` claims of the rows
that quote one with a path under `crates`. 46 returned another count; each was
read. Twenty rows (and five of [`appkit-gap.md`](appkit-gap.md)) changed
status and carry "(was …)" in the status cell; the others matched code of the
macOS platform layer or the docs, and stay. The counts and the per-part
tables below are still the snapshot of `b52708df`; where a row and this
section disagree, this section is newer. The `rg` claims of `appkit-gap.md`
were re-run the same way.

## Steps

1. Read [Summary](#summary) and [Top 20](#top-20-gaps) to choose work.
2. Open the part of the area (see [Contents](#contents)). Each gap row says what
   an app does with the API, the Rust or Freya way, the size and the blocker.
3. To find one SwiftUI name, search this page: every name of the closed list
   appears once in the `Closed list` of its part.
4. When a commit closes a gap, delete its row here and change the matrix row in
   the same commit (`AGENTS.md`, «Documentation», rule 3).

## Method and counts

The closed list comes from a script over the two interfaces (not committed).
It reads every top-level `public` declaration, every member of an `extension`
and the `@available` lines above them.

| Item | Count |
|---|---|
| Top-level public types, protocols, typealiases and macros, no leading `_` | 897 |
| Of these, unavailable on macOS (`@available(macOS, unavailable)`) | 75 |
| Nested public types (lower bound; classified with their owner) | 180 |
| Modifiers: 444 in `extension View`, 31 in `extension Scene` | 475 |
| Static style members (`extension XStyle where Self == Y`) | 177 |
| Environment values (`extension EnvironmentValues`) | 123 |
| **Items classified one by one** | **1,672** |

The brief spoke of about 1,042 types. The script finds 897 top-level names and at
least 180 nested ones, so the figures are close; the difference is how nested
types and duplicate `#if` branches are counted. Modifiers of other extensions
(`Text`, `Font`, `Path`, `Shape`, `TabContent`, the `init` overloads of
`TextField`, `List` or `Picker`) are not classified one by one: each part reads
them through their owner type.

Nine area reviews split the list by name patterns. Each review read the
interface lines, grepped the kit (`crates/`, the glossary, `agents/`) and the
Freya and Skia sources, and wrote its part. A script checked that every name of
the closed list is classified once. The only gap in the first pass was
`dynamicTypeSize` (modifier and environment value), which two reviews each left
to the other; this study classifies both as `partial` (matrix §17: only `Text`
and `SidebarItem` scale).

These claims were re-run by `grep` for this document and held: `FocusState`
absent, no `Label` view, no `Binding`, no `Spacer`, `List::new` takes
`Vec<String>`, the status item sets only an icon name, `wp_cursor_shape_v1` is
bound, `bk::prelude` re-exports only `controls::*` and `styles::*`, and
`CivilDate::today()` uses the UTC date. Every other claim rests on its review's
own `grep`, which the row quotes; the reviews list what they could not verify.
No probe ran, so no row adds a macOS measurement.

## How to read the tables

Status words (the [matrix](swiftui-appkit-coverage.md#statuses) uses a
different four-word scale for measured looks; this one rates API coverage):

| Status | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `implemented` | The kit has a public item that does the same job. The row names it. |
| `partial` | The kit has something nearby; the note says what is missing. |
| `absent` | The kit has nothing and the feature applies on Linux and Wayland. A `grep` backs it. |
| `n/a` | It does not apply: the SDK marks it unavailable on macOS, or it needs hardware or a service Linux lacks. The reason is given. |
| `out of scope` | The project decided not to cover it. Needs an ADR. |

Only 7 items are `out of scope`, all under [ADR 0083](../decisions.md#0083--what-the-kit-does-not-cover).
Items an owner could still exclude are `absent` with `candidate out of scope`
in the note ([decision 1](#decisions-for-the-owner)).

Size, for an `absent` or `partial` row:

| Size | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `S` | Under about 150 lines in one module; no new dependency or protocol. |
| `M` | 150 to 800 lines, a new module, or one new Wayland protocol. |
| `L` | Over 800 lines, or new compositor work, a Freya patch, a shader pipeline or a GPU path. |

Blocker: `none`; `Freya` (what is missing, with the file); `Wayland protocol`
(name, and whether the pinned crates carry it); `compositor` (malaysia must do
it); `shader`; `GPU`; `measurement` (a probe on macOS comes first); `design`
(no macOS reference to copy). `Since` is the macOS version that introduced the
API when it is 26 or 27. Matrix references are written `matrix §N`.

## Summary

Items per status. An item is a type, a modifier, a static style member or an
environment value.

| Part | Items | implemented | partial | absent | n/a | out of scope |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Controls and styles | 184 | 48 | 79 | 50 | 7 | 0 |
| 2. Text, fields and search | 136 | 8 | 49 | 69 | 10 | 0 |
| 3. Containers, lists, tables, forms, layout | 255 | 41 | 63 | 129 | 22 | 0 |
| 4. Navigation, tabs, toolbar, presentation | 219 | 16 | 53 | 95 | 54 | 1 |
| 5. App, scenes, windows, commands, documents | 217 | 11 | 60 | 60 | 86 | 0 |
| 6. Input | 182 | 3 | 40 | 87 | 52 | 0 |
| 7. State, environment, previews | 104 | 5 | 44 | 40 | 11 | 4 |
| 8. Animation, graphics, materials | 285 | 24 | 78 | 177 | 4 | 2 |
| 9. Accessibility | 90 | 1 | 27 | 24 | 38 | 0 |
| **All** | **1,672** | **157** | **493** | **731** | **284** | **7** |

By kind: types 897 (76 / 238 / 385 / 194 / 4), modifiers 475 (29 / 165 / 212 /
67 / 2), static style members 177 (48 / 53 / 71 / 5 / 0), environment values 123
(4 / 37 / 63 / 18 / 1).

What the numbers say:

- **The looks are there; the models are not.** The kit draws the controls
  (matrix §1 to §6, §11 and §12). What is missing are the declarative models an
  app is written in: bindings and environment, scenes and commands, navigation
  and toolbars, gestures, transitions, accessibility modifiers.
- **Containers hold text, not views.** `List` and `Table` take `Vec<String>`,
  90 control signatures take `&'static str`, and there is no `Label`. Real
  rows need views ([top 20](#top-20-gaps), 1 and 2).
- **Five blockers recur in Freya 0.5.3.** One handler per event per element
  (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/element.rs:481`); a flat renderer with no subtree
  layers (`src/render_pipeline.rs:59-207`); a single-pass layout in Torin; a node
  label that Freya overwrites (`create_node`); no text runs for the accessibility
  tree. The kit already writes its own `ElementExt` elements, which bypasses
  some of them.
- **Wayland blockers are few.** `zwp_pointer_gestures_v1` is unbound and
  malaysia does not advertise it; a client cannot grab a popup without an input
  serial; file dialogs need `xdg-foreign` and malaysia has no exporter; the
  compositor ignores the drag icon; a client cannot place its window.
- **The pinned crates already carry protocols the kit leaves unused:**
  `xdg-session-management`, `xdg-toplevel-icon`, `xdg-toplevel-tag`,
  `xdg-foreign` in `wayland-protocols` 0.32.13, features `staging` and
  `unstable` on in `Cargo.toml`.
- **The macOS 26 and 27 news is mostly absent** ([the section](#new-in-macos-26-and-27)).
  A third of it is visionOS or Apple-hardware API.

## Findings that are not API gaps

Bugs, name clashes and documentation drift found on the way. The area parts
repeat the ones they own.

**A bug.** `CivilDate::today()` (`crates/bk/src/date.rs:141`) divides the Unix
time by 86,400, so it returns the UTC date. The date picker's Today button is
wrong near midnight in every other zone. `ctappearance` already has a private
`localtime_r` helper (`config.rs:381`, per the state review).

**Docs and code that disagree.**

- `docs/barrierkit/04-appearance-and-tokens.md:322` shows an app importing
  `ctappearance::ColorScheme`, against ADR 0061 (an app depends only on `bk`);
  `bk::prelude` does not re-export it.
- `docs/research/styling-apple.md:397` says the user settings come from
  `org.freedesktop.appearance`. `grep -rniE 'org\.freedesktop\.appearance' crates` finds
  nothing; the kit reads only malaysia's `appearance.domain`.
- `docs/barrierkit/18-platform.md` does not list `xdg_wm_dialog_v1`, which the kit
  binds (`crates/bk/src/platform/linux/dialog.rs`).
- `AGENTS.md` says an app starts with `use bk::prelude::*;`. The prelude
  re-exports `controls::*` and `styles::*`, so `PopUpButton`, `RadioGroup`,
  `Stepper`, `ProgressView`, `Link`, `DatePicker`, `ColorPicker`, `Gauge`,
  `ControlGroup` and a few more need a module path.

**Names that clash with SwiftUI.** `app::Scene` means one window, while SwiftUI's
`Scene` is a protocol. `styles::MenuStyle` is a popup palette and
`buttons::LabelStyle` a two-case enum, while SwiftUI has protocols of the same
names. `MenuButton` is the deprecated SwiftUI name of `Menu`.
Glossary rule 3 asks for a rename when the item is touched.

**Matrix rows to change** (the matrix is not edited here):

| Row | Matrix says | This study says |
|---|---|---|
| §17 VoiceOver | `implemented` | `partial`: 10 views accept `a11y_*`; no Text interface; only Focus and Click actions are routed |
| §13 `Commands`, `CommandMenu`; `MenuBarExtra` | `implemented` | `partial`: no standard menus or placements; the status item is tied to a window and has no pixmap |
| §14 `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `Settings` | `implemented` | `partial`: one window per `Application`; nothing opens `Settings` |
| §12 `LabeledContent`, `ContentUnavailableView` | `implemented` | `partial` (was no description, actions or search variant): `ContentUnavailableView` has `description`, `actions` and `search(query)`; `LabeledContent` is `Row` |
| §12 `LazyVGrid`, `LazyHGrid` | `implemented` | `partial`: `CollectionView` is an icon grid with no `GridItem` |
| §12 `ZStack`, `ViewThatFits`, `Layout` (one row) | `partial` | `implemented` (was `partial`; [Custom layout](../barrierkit/11-views.md#custom-layout)): `ZStack`, `overlay`, `background_view`, `ViewThatFits` (explicit extent), `Layout` (placement only) |
| §7 `findNavigator` | `implemented` | `partial`: no binding, no replace, `TextEditor` only |
| §6 `Gauge` | seven styles `implemented` | `.circular` and `.linear` are unavailable on macOS; `.accessoryLinear` is absent |
| §1 `EditButton`; §8 `MultiDatePicker` | `missing` | `n/a`: unavailable on macOS |
| §8 time parts "no seconds" | `partial` | seconds exist only in AppKit; the SwiftUI component is watchOS-only |
| §13 `.dropDestination` "only on the image well" | `partial` | any element takes file drops; the limit is that only `text/uri-list` is accepted |
| §15 `GlassEffectContainer` | `implemented` | `partial`: a row of at most 8 shapes; `ButtonStyle::glass` is a flat client fill |
| §17 Motion | timings observed | nothing measured a SwiftUI `Animation` preset |
| §18 `ToolbarMinimizationBehavior` and four more | gaps | on 27.2 the SDK exposes only `.automatic`; classified `n/a` |
| §7 `.roundedBorder`, `.squareBorder` | current | deprecated in macOS 27 in favor of `.bordered` and `textInputBorderShape` |

The matrix has no section for macOS 26 and none for state and environment;
each part proposes rows.

## New in macOS 26 and 27

Items whose first `@available` is macOS 26 (137 items) or 27 (67). The
count is by item, as above; member-level additions (an `init` of an old type) are
not counted, except these macOS 27 ones: `alert(error:)`, `AsyncImage(request:)`,
`TabRole.prominent`, `DynamicViewContent.reorderable()`, `reorderDestination`,
`GeometryProxy.reservedRegions` and `onHingeChange`. All are `absent`.
A scan found 307 member-level declarations for 26 (mostly `init` overloads and
conformances) that this study did not read one by one.

The matrix §18 lists 13 macOS 27 entries. The closed list has more: the whole
document model (`Document`, readers, writers, creation types), `GestureInputKinds`,
`AnyNavigationTransition`, `ReservedRegion`, `DeviceHinge`, the reorder types and
`alert(error:)`. The 27.1 and 27.2 items
(`ArrangementView`, `ContentMarginGuide`, `ToolbarTitleAlignment`) are in the
list below with the rest.

### macOS 27 (67 items)

**types**

- partial (2): `TextInputBorderShape`, `ToolbarTitleAlignment`
- absent (27): `AnyNavigationTransition`, `ArrangementView`, `ArrangementViewStyle`, `ArrangementViewStyleConfiguration`, `AutomaticArrangementViewStyle`, `BorderedTextFieldStyle`, `ContentMarginGuide`, `DefaultNewDocumentButtonLabel`, `Document`, `DocumentCreationContext`, `DocumentCreationSource`, `DocumentReadConfiguration`, `DocumentReader`, `DocumentWriteConfiguration`, `DocumentWriter`, `FileWrapperDocumentReader`, `FileWrapperDocumentWriter`, `GestureInputKinds`, `OverlayArrangementViewStyle`, `ReadableDocument`, `ReorderDifference`, `ReorderableSingleCollectionIdentifier`, `ReservedRegion`, `SplitArrangementViewStyle`, `TabsPickerStyle`, `ToolbarVerticalBehavior`, `WritableDocument`
- n/a (9): `DeviceHinge`, `DeviceHingeContext`, `PresentationPlacement`, `TabSectionExpansion`, `ToolbarItemAxisBehavior`, `ToolbarMinimizationBehavior`, `ToolbarMinimizationRestoration`, `ToolbarMinimizationSafeAreaAdjustment`, `ToolbarVerticalCompressionBehavior`

**view modifiers**

- partial (1): `textInputBorderShape`
- absent (9): `arrangementViewStyle`, `defaultTabBarPlacement`, `overlayArrangementEdge`, `reorderContainer`, `splitArrangementFixedLayoutSize`, `splitArrangementLayoutRatio`, `splitArrangementLayoutSize`, `swipeActionsContainer`, `toolbarVerticalBehavior`
- n/a (6): `onHingeChange`, `presentationPlacement`, `toolbarMinimizationBehavior`, `toolbarMinimizationRestoration`, `toolbarMinimizationSafeAreaAdjustment`, `toolbarVerticalCompressionBehavior`
- out of scope (1): `asyncImageURLSession`

**static style members**

- absent (6): `ArrangementViewStyle.automatic`, `ArrangementViewStyle.overlay`, `ArrangementViewStyle.split`, `PickerStyle.tabs`, `Shape.textInputBorder`, `TextFieldStyle.bordered`

**environment values**

- partial (1): `toolbarTitleAlignment`
- absent (5): `isTabViewSidebarAvailable`, `overlayArrangementZIndex`, `splitArrangementAxis`, `systemPrefersReducedResourceUsage`, `toolbarVerticalEdge`


### macOS 26 (137 items)

Thirty-five types, six environment values and a few modifiers are visionOS,
widget, immersive-space or `AppKit` hosting API; they are `n/a`.

**types**

- implemented (2): `GlassButtonStyle`, `GlassProminentButtonStyle`
- partial (7): `ButtonSizing`, `DefaultGlassEffectShape`, `Glass`, `GlassEffectContainer`, `ScrollEdgeEffectStyle`, `SearchToolbarBehavior`, `SliderTick`
- absent (29): `AnimatableValues`, `AttributedTextFormatting`, `AttributedTextFormattingDefinition`, `AttributedTextSelection`, `AttributedTextValueConstraint`, `ConcentricRectangle`, `DefaultButtonLabel`, `DefaultToolbarItem`, `DragConfiguration`, `DragDropPreviewsFormation`, `DragSession`, `DropConfiguration`, `DropSession`, `FindContext`, `GlassEffectTransition`, `RectangleCornerInsets`, `RoundedRectangularShape`, `RoundedRectangularShapeCorners`, `SliderTickBuilder`, `SliderTickContent`, `SliderTickContentForEach`, `SpacerSizing`, `SymbolColorRenderingMode`, `SymbolVariableValueMode`, `TabSearchActivation`, `TextInputFormattingControlPlacement`, `ToolbarItemVisibilityPriority`, `ToolbarSpacer`, `TupleSliderTickContent`

## Top 20 gaps

Ranking by how much a gap limits what an app designer can write, not by
effort. Sizes and blockers come from the parts. The part number points to the
row.

| # | Gap | Why it limits apps | Size | Blocker | Part | Now (2026-11-22) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Views in `List` and `Table` rows and cells | Mail, files and settings rows need avatars, badges and controls; today rows are `String`s, so `listRow*` has nothing to act on | M | none | 3 | **Done.** [Views in rows and cells](../barrierkit/11-views.md#views-in-rows-and-cells); `List::row`, `Table::cell` (`crates/bk/src/views/list.rs:61`, `table.rs:79`) |
| 2 | `Label`, owned strings and view-valued labels | 90 signatures take `&'static str` (runtime lists need `Box::leak`); no `Label`; only `Button` takes a symbol | M | none | 1 | **Mostly done.** `Label` (`elements.rs:239`, [Common rules](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#common-rules)); 13 public signatures still take `&'static str` |
| 3 | `Spacer`, `Grid`, `ViewThatFits`, a custom `Layout` | The first things a SwiftUI designer writes. `Spacer` and `frame(alignment:)` are S, `Grid` M, `Layout` L (placement-only M) | S to L | Freya: Torin single pass; `post_measure` helps | 3 | **Mostly done ([Custom layout](../barrierkit/11-views.md#custom-layout)).** `Spacer`, `Grid`, `Frame` ([Layout](../barrierkit/11-views.md#layout)); `ZStack`, `overlay`, `background_view`, `Layout` (placement only) and `ViewThatFits` (explicit extents) in `crates/bk/src/layout/`; several proposals, `AnyLayout` and a measured `ViewThatFits` stay open (`grep -rnE 'AnyLayout|size_that_fits' crates` → 0) |
| 4 | Multi-scene `App`: `WindowGroup(id:for:)`, `Window`, `Settings`, `openWindow` | One window per `Application`; editors, inspectors and per-item windows cannot be written | M | none | 5 | **Done.** `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `Settings`, `use_open_window` (`app/windows.rs`, [Scenes](../barrierkit/08-windows.md#scenes)) |
| 5 | `Commands` and the standard menus | Quit, Close, Cut, Copy, Paste, Find, Window and Help are written by hand; no placements | M | measurement | 5 | **Done, opt-in.** `Commands`, `CommandGroup` (`app/commands.rs`, [Standard menus](../barrierkit/13-app.md#standard-menus)) |
| 6 | `NavigationStack` with a typed path | Only `History` and `Row::page` (settings only); every multi-screen app needs push and pop | M | none (Freya has no router in the pinned set) | 4 | **Done.** `NavigationStack`, `NavigationLink` (`views/navigation_stack.rs`, recipe `navigation_stack.rs`) |
| 7 | Declarative toolbar: `ToolbarItem(placement:)`, `ToolbarSpacer`, labels, overflow priority | The title bar has two slots and icon-only items | M | measurement | 4 | **Partly closed ([Toolbar items](../barrierkit/09-titlebars.md#toolbar-items)).** `Titlebar::toolbar` takes `ToolbarItem` and `ToolbarSpacer` by placement; labels and overflow priority stay open |
| 8 | Binding-driven `sheet`, `alert`, `confirmationDialog`, `popover` | No `isPresented`, `item` or `onDismiss`; the app adds and removes components | M | Wayland: a popup grab needs an input serial | 4 | **Done for sheet, alert, popover** (ADR 0085, [Sheet](../barrierkit/11-views.md#sheet)); `item:` and `onDismiss` forms open |
| 9 | `Binding` | Controls take a value and a callback; every form pays a closure per control; `Writable<T>` is the base | M | none | 7 | **Declined.** ADR 0085: no `Binding` |
| 10 | Ambient `disabled` and `isEnabled` | 17 controls have their own flag, 8 none, no container can disable its children | M | none | 1, 7 | **Done.** `.disabled(bool)`, `use_is_enabled()` ([Disabled](../barrierkit/16-swiftui-to-the-kit.md#disabled)) |
| 11 | App-defined environment values and `.environment(_:_:)` | Contexts are type-keyed and not reactive; an app cannot pass a model down simply | S | none | 7 | **Done.** `.environment(value)`, `use_environment` (`environment.rs`) |
| 12 | `keyboardShortcut`, `FocusState`, `defaultFocus` | Shortcuts work only through a `MainMenu` item; focus cannot move by name | S each | none | 6 | **Done for buttons.** `Button::keyboard_shortcut`, `FocusState` ([Focus](../barrierkit/16-swiftui-to-the-kit.md#focus)); a shortcut on another view is open |
| 13 | The `Gesture` API: `.gesture`, composition, long press, `onTapGesture(count:)` | Custom interaction is written as gestures; each control hand-rolls pointer tracking | L | Freya: one handler per event, no press capture | 6 | **Done.** `gesture.rs`, [Gestures](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#gestures), recipe `gestures.rs` |
| 14 | Typed drag and drop | Only `text/uri-list` is accepted; text, images and items cannot be dropped | M | Freya: file-only drop event (bypassable) | 6 | **Partial (was: files and text only).** `DragPayload::{Files, Text, Html, Image}`, `Transferable`, `Pasteboard` and the macOS drag session exist ([Typed clipboard](../barrierkit/13-app.md#typed-clipboard)); a typed drop target does not |
| 15 | Text model: `Text + Text`, Markdown, `textSelection`, SwiftUI-named modifiers | A label cannot hold one bold word; Freya has most modifiers but `bk` publishes none by SwiftUI name | S to M | none | 2 | **Done (was open).** `Text + Text`, `Text::markdown`, `Text::text_selection` and `bold`, `italic`, `underline`, `strikethrough` ([Text runs](../barrierkit/06-text-and-editing.md#text-runs)); `kerning`, `tracking` and `baselineOffset` stay absent |
| 16 | Animation presets, `withAnimation`, `Transaction`, `Transition`, `matchedGeometryEffect` | The kit has one fixed spring inside controls and a private `use_animated`; state-driven animation is the SwiftUI default | M each | measurement, design | 7, 8 | **Partial.** `with_animation`, `Animation`, springs ([Spring](../barrierkit/14-animations.md#spring)); `Transition` and `matchedGeometryEffect` open (grep → 0) |
| 17 | `Shape` views, `Path`, `ConcentricRectangle` | No drawable shape that lays out like a view; the macOS 26 geometry is concentric (rule measured 2026-11-22, [macOS corners](macos-corners.md#7-the-concentric-rule)) | M | none | 8 | **Open.** No `Shape` views or `Path`; the concentric rule exists as a function ([Corners](../barrierkit/04-appearance-and-tokens.md#corners)) |
| 18 | `accessibility*` modifiers on every control; text-input accessibility | Only 10 views accept `a11y_*`; Orca cannot read the caret or selection of a field | M (modifiers), L (text) | Freya: label overwrite; no text runs | 9 | **Open.** Per-control `a11y_*` only; no SwiftUI-named accessibility modifiers |
| 19 | Scroll API: two axes, `ScrollViewReader`, `scrollPosition`, geometry and phase, bounce | The controller moves by offset on one axis | M | none (bounce: measurement) | 3 | **Partial.** Scroll physics ([Scrolling](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#scrolling)) and `ScrollController`; `scrollPosition` and `scrollTargetBehavior` open |
| 20 | `glassEffect` on any view and subtree effects (`blur`, `blendMode`, `mask`, `layerEffect`) | `GlassContainer` is a row only; one Freya render-pipeline patch would unlock about 15 modifiers | M (glass), L (patch) | measurement; Freya | 8 | **Open.** `GlassContainer` only; no `glass_effect` modifier (ADR 0092, 0100) |

Next in line, not ranked: `DocumentGroup` and the macOS 27 `Document` model (L,
needs rows 4, 5 and the lifecycle hooks); app lifecycle hooks (S); search
suggestions, scopes and completion (M); `TextField(axis: .vertical)` and
formats (M); a content column and a visibility binding in `NavigationSplitView`
(M); `timeZone`, `calendar` and `locale` values (M); `MenuBarExtra` without a
window (M); an Xcode-preview equivalent (static M, hot reload L); safe areas and
`contentMargins` (M); file dialogs modal to the window (compositor:
`xdg-foreign`); custom style bodies, `makeBody` (M per protocol); labels and
`total` for `ProgressView` and `Gauge` (M).

## Decisions for the owner

1. **What is out of scope.** [ADR 0083](../decisions.md#0083--what-the-kit-does-not-cover)
   excludes Core Data, URL loading and HDR (7 items). It keeps `Shader` and
   `MeshGradient` in scope, because Skia runs them, and leaves the document
   model undecided: the matrix scope sentence excludes it, but backlog D.7 plans
   document scenes. Rows still marked `candidate out of scope` are not covered by the ADR.
2. **Owned strings and view-valued labels.** [Common rules](../barrierkit/05-controls.md#common-rules)
   sets `impl Into<Cow<'static, str>>` for text, `&'static str` for ids and a
   `label` builder for views. The text change is source compatible, so it is not
   breaking; each control added in the old form adds one signature to change.
3. **How to treat Freya.** ADR 0033 pins `=0.5.3` and bans `freya-winit`. Gestures
   (row 13), subtree effects (row 20) and accessibility modifiers (row 18) are
   `L` only because of Freya. The choice is a patched fork, kit-owned
   `ElementExt` elements for each case, or accepting the limit.


## Parts by area

Each part starts with the counts and what dominates the gaps. Then: the gap
table, the closed list, the area's priorities and the drift and unverified
items. The tables are the reviews' text; the numbers `matrix §N` point to the
[coverage matrix](swiftui-appkit-coverage.md).

## 1. Controls and their styles

The closed list has 111 types, 19 view modifiers, 47 static style members and 7 environment values, plus 3 members added to own types. Types: 27 implemented, 44 partial, 33 absent, 7 n/a; modifiers: 1 implemented, 13 partial, 5 absent; static style members: 20 implemented, 19 partial, 8 absent; environment values: 3 partial, 4 absent. The built-in looks are mostly there (matrix §1-§6, §11); the gaps are structural: no custom style body, no `Label`, no view-valued or dynamic labels, no container-level `disabled`, no label views on `ProgressView`, `Gauge` and `Slider`, and the macOS 26 and 27 additions. Freya gives `provide_context` and `try_consume_context` (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/lifecycle/context.rs:77,160`), which the kit already uses for `Theme` and which is the way to add scope values such as `disabled` or `labels_visibility`. The input missed `ToggleStyle.switch` (implemented as `Switch`; section 4).

### 1.1 Gaps and discrepancies

#### Custom style protocols: verdict per protocol, with evidence

- Evidence for all: `ControlStyle` has only `tengen()` and `accessible()` (`crates/bk/src/styles/theme.rs:14-20`). It has no render method. `resolve`, `Paints`, `Painted` and `control_state` are `pub(crate)` (`crates/bk/src/styles.rs:608,627`, `crates/bk/src/interaction.rs:98`), so an app can implement `ControlStyle` for its own struct and chain it in a `Theme`, but nothing reads it and an app-written view cannot join the cascade. Each kit control reads only its own struct (`crates/bk/src/controls/button.rs`: `let style: ButtonStyle = resolve(&self.style)`).
- `ButtonStyle` and `PrimitiveButtonStyle`: `partial`. The app can change the 13 fields of `ButtonStyle` (height, padding, shape, per-state background and label paint, border, shadows, opacity, pressed scale, color effect, font, animation) at four levels (Tengen, `Theme`, `.control_style`, `.style`). The app cannot replace the body, add or move label parts, read the label or the role, call `trigger()`, or use another hit shape. `Button` takes a `String` and an optional `Symbol` only.
- `ToggleStyle`: `partial`. `SwitchStyle` and `CheckboxStyle` are separate structs of two controls; same limits. There is no `Toggle` type to swap a style on.
- `ProgressViewStyle`: `partial`. Three structs for three controls (`ProgressViewStyle` bar, `ProgressRingStyle`, `SpinnerStyle`); no custom body, no configuration (`fractionCompleted`, `label`, `currentValueLabel`).
- `GaugeStyle`: `partial`. One struct (`GaugeStyle`, ring metrics) plus `CapacityIndicatorStyle`; no custom body.
- `MenuStyle`: `partial`, and a name clash. The kit's `MenuStyle` is `ctdesign::MenuPalette`, the colors of the popup panel, not the look of the menu button. The button is `MenuButtonStyle`.
- `ControlGroupStyle`: `partial`. One struct, one look (rounded group with hairlines); no `.menu`, `.palette`, `.navigation` look.
- `DatePickerStyle`: `partial`. One struct plus `DatePicker::graphical()` and `field()`; no `.compact`; no custom body (`makeBody` exists since macOS 13).
- `LabelStyle`: `partial`, and there is no protocol and no `Label` to style. `buttons::LabelStyle` is a two-case enum (`IconOnly`, `TitleAndIcon`) for `MenuButton` only.
- `PickerStyle`: SwiftUI itself offers no public body (`PickerStyle` has only underscore requirements, `SwiftUI.swiftinterface:16726`), so an app cannot write one there either. Kit: three controls plus their style structs. `partial` only because the kit has no `Picker` front type.
- `MenuButtonStyle` (deprecated protocol): same, underscore only.
- What an app can do today: restyle any kit control per state, build a whole `Theme`, and draw its own component with Freya's `rect()` and the public `use_interaction()` and `Palette::current()`, but without `ShapeStyle`, `StateValue`, themes or `ControlSize`.
- What an app cannot do: write `makeBody(configuration:)` for any control; give a custom style to a `.control_style` scope; reuse kit styling in its own control.

### 1.3 Area priorities

1. Dynamic text in controls: 90 public signatures take `&'static str` (`PopUpButton::item`, `Slider::new`, `Switch::new`, `SegmentedControl::segment`, `Link::new`, `ProgressView::*` ...), so a list built at run time needs `Box::leak`. Every real app with a runtime list hits it. Size M; Blocker none.
2. `Label` and view-valued labels: no `Label`, and only `Button` takes a symbol next to its text; no control takes a view. Toolbars, menus, lists and settings rows all need text plus symbol. Size M; Blocker none.
3. Custom style bodies (`ButtonStyle.makeBody`): ADR 0064 limits an app to 13 fields of `ButtonStyle`; it cannot change the structure of a button, a toggle or a progress bar. Size M for the button, then M per protocol; Blocker none.
4. Container `.disabled(_)`: 17 controls have their own flag and 8 have none (`MenuButton`, `ComboButton`, `Link`, `HelpLink`, `ControlGroup`, `ToggleGroup`, `ToolGroup`, `ProgressView`); a panel cannot be disabled in one call. Size M; Blocker none (a context, like `StyleScope`).
5. `ProgressView` and `Gauge` content: visible label, value label, `total`, `timerInterval`, min and max labels. Download and meter rows cannot be built without composing text by hand. Size M; Blocker none.
6. `Link(destination:)` with the pointer cursor: today a `Link` opens nothing by itself. Size S; Blocker none.
7. macOS 26 `Slider` ticks, `neutralValue`, `enabledBounds`, `sliderThumbVisibility`: balance and range sliders cannot be drawn. Size M; Blocker measurement (a probe of the macOS 26 `Slider`).
8. `ButtonRole` (cancel, confirm, close, destructive) with `Menu` and `ControlGroup` styles: dialogs and toolbars need the system looks; no probe of SwiftUI `Menu` styles exists. Size M; Blocker measurement.

### 1.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified

Rows the matrix lacks (proposed as SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):

- Custom style bodies: `ButtonStyle.makeBody`, `ToggleStyle.makeBody`, `ProgressViewStyle`, `GaugeStyle`, `MenuStyle`, `ControlGroupStyle`, `DatePickerStyle` | `NSCell` subclass | `ControlStyle` (no body) | partial | P1.
- Dynamic text in 90 control signatures (`&'static str`) | `NSString` | `PopUpButton::item`, `Slider::new`, ... | partial | P1.
- Container `.disabled(_)`, `isEnabled` | `NSControl.isEnabled` | 17 per-control `disabled` | partial | P1.
- View-valued labels | `NSButton.attributedTitle`, `image` | `Button::system_image` | partial | P1.
- `Label` split from `LabelStyle` (the matrix row `Label, LabelStyle` is P2) | `NSTextField` + `NSImageView` | none | missing | P1 (the building block of toolbars, menus, rows).
- Visible label of `Slider`, `Picker`, `Stepper`, `DatePicker`, `ColorPicker`, and `labelsVisibility(_)` | `NSTextField(labelWithString:)` beside the control | `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `RadioGroup` draw it; others a11y only | partial | P2.
- `Link(destination:)` | `NSTextField` with a link attribute | `Link::on_press` | partial | P2.
- `ProgressView` label, `currentValueLabel`, `total`, `timerInterval` | `NSProgressIndicator.minValue`, `maxValue` | `ProgressView` | partial | P2.
- `Gauge` `in:` range, min and max labels, `tint(Gradient)` | `NSLevelIndicator.minValue`, `maxValue` | `Gauge`, `CapacityIndicator` | partial | P2.
- macOS 26 additions (the matrix §18 covers macOS 27 only): `SliderTick` family, `neutralValue`, `enabledBounds`, `sliderThumbVisibility`, `ButtonSizing`, `Button(role:)` with `DefaultButtonLabel`, `ButtonRole.confirm` and `.close`, `labelIconToTitleSpacing`, `labelReservedIconWidth`, `ControlSize` `Comparable` and `controlSize(range)` | `NSControl.ControlSize` | none | missing | P2 to P3. Done in the kit: `.glass`, `.glassProminent`.
- `ControlGroup` styles `.menu`, `.compactMenu`, `.palette` and `NavigationControlGroupStyle` | `NSToolbarItemGroup` representation | `ControlGroup` one look | partial | P2.
- `Stepper` step, `Double` value, `onIncrement`/`onDecrement` | `NSStepper.increment` | `Stepper` (`i64`, step 1) | partial | P3.
- `RenameButton`, `MenuOrder`, `PaletteSelectionEffect`, `.badgeProminence(_)` | — | none | missing | P3.
- Deprecated SwiftUI names the matrix does not list: `MenuButton`, `MenuButtonStyle` family, `PopUpButtonPickerStyle`, `PullDownButton` | — | `MenuButton`, `PopUpButton::pulls_down` | implemented / candidate out of scope | P3.

Rows this study disagrees with:

- Matrix §6 row `Gauge` (`.linearCapacity`, `.accessoryLinear`, `.accessoryLinearCapacity`, `.accessoryCircular`, `.accessoryCircularCapacity`, `.circular`, `.linear`) is `implemented`. The SDK marks `.circular` and `.linear` unavailable on macOS (`CircularGaugeStyle`, `LinearGaugeStyle`, `SwiftUI.swiftinterface:13803,27778`). `.accessoryLinear` is absent (`grep -rniE 'accessory_?linear' crates` → 0). The kit's `Gauge` and `Gauge::capacity` draw `.accessoryCircular` and `.accessoryCircularCapacity`. `.linearCapacity` and `.accessoryLinearCapacity` map only to `CapacityIndicator`, the AppKit level indicator, whose look was never compared with SwiftUI's. The row should be `partial`.
- Matrix §1 row `PasteButton`, `EditButton`, `SettingsLink` is `missing`. `EditButton` is `macOS, unavailable` (`SwiftUI.swiftinterface:27745`), so `n/a`. `PasteButton` is in the E_input file and `SettingsLink` in D_scenes.
- Matrix §8 row `time parts` says `partial: no seconds`. SwiftUI's `DatePickerComponents.hourMinuteAndSecond` is watchOS only (`macOS, unavailable`, `SwiftUI.swiftinterface:26121`); seconds exist in AppKit's `NSDatePicker` only.
- Matrix §8 row `MultiDatePicker` is `missing`. The SDK marks it `macOS, unavailable`, so `n/a`.
- Matrix §11 rows `.controlSize(_:)` are `measured`. True for the 13 styles in `Theme::control_size`, but the scope skips `MenuButton`, `ComboButton`, `ControlGroup`, `HelpLink`, `ProgressRing`, `Spinner`, `ToggleGroup`, `IconButton`, `ColorPicker`, so a toolbar sized by `.control_size` stays regular.
- Matrix §1 rows `.plain`, `.link` are `partial` for an 8% pixel difference. This study classifies by API job (`implemented`); `.borderless` stays `partial` (hit area, agrees with the matrix).
- Matrix §4 row `Stepper` with label and value is `implemented`. Agreed for the look; the API is integer-only with step 1 (my `partial`).
- Matrix §12 row `Label`, `LabelStyle`: confirmed. `buttons::LabelStyle` (`crates/bk/src/buttons.rs:20`) exists for `MenuButton` only.
- Matrix §18 says the kit has none of the macOS 27 APIs. For this area that holds: `PickerStyle.tabs` / `TabsPickerStyle` → 0 hits.

Naming drift (AGENTS.md naming rule 2):

- The kit's `MenuButton` is the deprecated SwiftUI name; the glossary maps it to `Menu`. Candidate rename to `Menu` (glossary row, `crates/bk/src/buttons.rs:26`).
- Name clashes with SwiftUI protocols of different meaning: `styles::MenuStyle` is the popup palette (SwiftUI `MenuStyle` styles the menu button); `styles::MenuButtonStyle` is the menu button look (SwiftUI's deprecated `MenuButtonStyle` protocol); `buttons::LabelStyle` is a two-case enum (SwiftUI's protocol).
- `bk::prelude` re-exports only `controls::*` (`crates/bk/src/prelude.rs`). `PopUpButton`, `RadioGroup`, `Stepper`, `ProgressView`, `Link`, `MenuButton`, `DatePicker`, `ColorPicker`, `Gauge`, `ControlGroup`, `ShareLink`, `HelpLink` need their module path (`crates/bk/examples/styled_control.rs` imports `bk::level::LevelSlider`), against the first rule of AGENTS.md ("an app starts with `use bk::prelude::*`").

Misfiled or missing in the input:

- `AutomaticTableStyle`, `BorderedTableStyle` belong to B_containers (tables; kit `TableStyle`, matrix §12). This study classified them `implemented` from matrix §12 row `Table` `.automatic`, `.bordered`.
- `ToggleStyle.switch` is missing from the static members (the extractor skips the backticked keyword, `SwiftUI.swiftinterface:31581`). `GlassButtonStyle` and `GlassProminentButtonStyle` (`SwiftUI.swiftinterface:1757,4705`) and `NavigationControlGroupStyle` (`:21252`) are missing from the types.
- `PasteButton` (E_input), `SettingsLink` (D_scenes), `LabeledContent` (B_containers; the kit's `Row`, matrix §12 row `LabeledContent`) are in other area files. `.tint`, `.badge`, `.labelsHidden` and `.disabled` are not all in this file: `labelsVisibility` and `disabled` are; `.tint` (kit: `tint(Tint)` on `Slider`, `Switch`, `Checkbox`, `RadioGroup`, `ProgressView`, `LevelSlider` only; no container `.tint`) and `.badge` are elsewhere.
- Types from other frameworks that this area needs: `Binding<Bool>` and `Binding<Date>` (kit: callbacks and `CivilDateTime`), `Transferable` (`ShareLink`, `SharePreview`), `Progress` (Foundation), `LocalizedStringKey`.

Unverified:

- The SwiftUI look of every `GaugeStyle`, of `.accessoryBar`, `.accessoryBarAction`, `.inline`, `.palette` and `.tabs` pickers, and of the `Menu` styles: no probe renders them (matrix "Probes to write first").
- Whether `MenuButtonStyle.chevron_size = 0` hides the indicator.
- Whether `DefaultGaugeStyle` is `.linearCapacity` on macOS, and whether `ProgressView()` with no value is the spinner (matrix §6 row says so).
- That the Linux desktop has no share portal (the kit's own doc says so; this study did not check the xdg-desktop-portal source).
- Whether `menuOrder` has any visible effect on macOS menus that open downward.
- `Freya` offers no ready-made disabled or environment scope beyond contexts: this study read `lifecycle/context.rs` only and did not search `freya-core` for an enabled flag.

## 2. Text, fields and search

Types (47): 4 implemented, 15 partial, 23 absent, 5 n/a; view modifiers (58): 1 implemented, 26 partial, 26 absent, 4 n/a, 1 left to the accessibility agent (`dynamicTypeSize`); static style members (15): 3 implemented, 2 partial, 10 absent; environment values (16): 4 partial, 10 absent, 1 n/a, 1 left to the accessibility agent. No item is `out of scope`: no ADR in `docs/decisions.md` excludes a text feature, so items an owner could exclude are `absent` with `candidate out of scope` in the note. The gaps are the text model (`Text` is one `label()` with one style: no `Text + Text`, Markdown, `AttributedString`, selection, `TextRenderer` or formats), the SwiftUI names of modifiers Freya already has, and search beyond the field (suggestions, scopes, completion, placement). Freya 0.5.3 gives for free: `letter_spacing`, `text_decoration`, `font_slant`, `font_weight`, `font_width`, `font_tabular` and `text_align` (Left, Right, Center, Justify, Start, End) with an inherited text style (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/data.rs:164`), `paragraph()` spans with inline children, `SelectableText` and `Input::multiline` (both unused by the kit), and in `freya-skia-safe-0.104.0` glyph runs through `Paragraph::visit`. Freya blocks text direction, locale, baseline shift, decoration color and style (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/elements/paragraph.rs:820-863` never sets them) and any hook into how a label paints.

### 2.1 Gaps and discrepancies
| SwiftUI API (family) | Since | Status | Kit today | Used for | Rust/Freya way | Size | Blocker |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Text + Text`, `Text(Image)`, text interpolating `Text` or `Image`, `Text(AttributedString)` | 26 for Image | partial (was absent): `Text + Text` exists, `Text(Image)`, interpolation and `Text(AttributedString)` do not | **Now:** `impl Add for Text` (`elements.rs`) concatenates the runs of both operands into one paragraph (`styled_text.rs`); each operand keeps its own `bold()`, `font`, `foreground_style`. SwiftUI itself deprecated `+` in macOS 26. **Before:** `Text::new(Cow<str>)` wrapped one `label()` with one style. `grep -rniE "attributed\|impl.*Add.*for Text" crates docs/barrierkit agents` → 0. Spans exist only inside `TextEditor`, `TextView` and menus. | One sentence with a bold word, a symbol or a colored run | Freya `paragraph()` with `Span` (each span has its own `TextStyleData`), and inline children for the image (`ChildrenExt for Paragraph`, `freya-core-0.5.3/src/elements/paragraph.rs:983`; `SelectableText::child`). Kit side: `Text::span(..)` and an `Add` impl that builds the paragraph. | M | none |
| Inline Markdown in `Text(LocalizedStringKey)` and `AttributedString(markdown:)` |  | partial (was absent): `Text::markdown` reads the inline subset; `tr!` and a literal `Text::new` do not | **Now:** `rich_text::markdown` (a 70-line parser, no new dependency; strong, emphasis, strikethrough, code, link, escapes) feeds `Text::markdown`; `Text::new` stays verbatim. **Before:** `grep -rniE "markdown\|pulldown" crates docs/barrierkit agents` → 0. `tr!` returns plain Fluent text (`i18n.rs:47`). | Bold, italic, code, strikethrough and links inside a translated string | Parse the inline subset (strong, emphasis, code, strikethrough, link) with `pulldown-cmark` (new dependency) into the spans of the row above. Links reuse the `OpenURI` path of `rich_text::link_at`. | M | none |
| `LocalizedStringKey`, `Text(_:tableName:bundle:comment:)`, string interpolation with specifiers |  | partial | `tr!` gives `&'static str` from Fluent (plurals and selectors work; `docs/barrierkit/17-languages.md`). Every label is `&'static str` (`TextField::new`, `SearchField::new`, `TextEditor::new`, `ContentUnavailableView::new`), so a dynamic label goes through `i18n::intern`, which leaks it (`i18n.rs:32`). | Labels that change at run time, localized with arguments | Accept `impl Into<Cow<'static, str>>` as `Text::new` does and keep `tr!` for ids. A `LocalizedStringKey` type is not needed with Fluent. | S | none |

### 2.2 Classification of every item (closed list)

**types (47)**
- types - implemented (4): `DefaultTextFieldStyle`, `PlainTextFieldStyle`, `RoundedBorderTextFieldStyle`, `SecureField`
- types - partial (15): `AutomaticTextEditorStyle`, `PlainTextEditorStyle`, `Font`, `LocalizedStringKey`, `SearchFieldPlacement`, `SearchToolbarBehavior`, `SubmitTriggers`, `Text`, `TextAlignment`, `TextEditor`, `TextEditorStyle`, `TextEditorStyleConfiguration`, `TextField`, `TextFieldStyle`, `TextInputBorderShape`

Members added by extensions to the area's own types (not in the four counts above):
- Font - partial: body, callout, caption, caption2, footnote, headline, largeTitle, subheadline, title, title2, title3 (the `ctdesign` constants BODY ... TITLE_3, matrix §7 text styles), monospaced (`MONO`), scaled (`Font::scaled(factor)`), weight (the `weight` field)
- Font - absent: bold, custom, italic, leading, lowercaseSmallCaps, monospacedDigit, pointSize, resolve, smallCaps, system, uppercaseSmallCaps, width
- Text - implemented: foregroundColor, foregroundStyle (`ViewModifiers::foreground_style` on a `Text`)
- Text - partial: bold, font, fontWeight, fontWidth, italic, kerning, monospaced, monospacedDigit, strikethrough, tracking, underline (Freya `TextStyleExt` methods with Freya names)
- Text - absent: baselineOffset, customAttribute, fontDesign, textScale, textVariant, typesettingLanguage
- Text - not classified here (accessibility agent): accessibilityHeading, accessibilityLabel, accessibilityTextContentType, speechAdjustedPitch, speechAlwaysIncludesPunctuation, speechAnnouncementsQueued, speechSpellsOutCharacters
- AttributedTextFormattingDefinition - absent: constrain
- AttributedTextSelection - absent: affinity, attributes, indices, typingAttributes
- AttributedTextValueConstraint - absent: body
- TextInputBorderShape - partial: inset, path (`Shape` has `corner_radius` only, no `path` or `inset`)
- TextRenderer - absent: displayPadding, sizeThatFits

### 2.3 Area priorities
1. `Text + Text`, Markdown in `Text`, `Text(Image)`: a label cannot hold one bold word, a symbol or a link, so every sentence with emphasis needs hand-built `paragraph()` code. Size M; Blocker none.
2. SwiftUI-named text modifiers (`bold`, `italic`, `underline`, `strikethrough`, `kerning`, `tracking`, `lineSpacing`, `multilineTextAlignment`, `truncationMode(.middle/.head)`, `textCase`, `minimumScaleFactor`): Freya already has most; the AGENTS naming rule says an app should write the SwiftUI name. Size S (M with `minimumScaleFactor`); Blocker none.
3. `textSelection(.enabled)`: copying an error, an address or a version is expected on macOS and Freya ships `SelectableText` unused (copy is not in it). Size S; Blocker none.
4. `TextField(axis: .vertical)` with `lineLimit(range)` and `TextField(value:format:)`: comment boxes and numeric fields are in nearly every form (matrix §7 P2, formats are new). Size M; Blocker none.
5. `searchSuggestions`, `searchScopes`, `searchCompletion`, `searchable(isPresented:placement:)`, and `textInputSuggestions`: search without a suggestions list or a scope bar is not a macOS search. Size M; Blocker none (measurement for the scope bar).
6. `TextSelection` binding for `TextField` and `TextEditor`: formatting, insert-at-caret and keeping the caret need it; the one-line `Input` hides its selection. Size M; Blocker Freya (`Input`).
7. Bidirectional text, `writingDirection`, `typesettingLanguage`: Freya never sets the Skia text direction or locale, so the paragraph base direction is Skia's default, left-to-right (not run here), which breaks alignment and punctuation in right-to-left apps. Size M; Blocker Freya (`ParagraphStyle` built without direction); a kit-owned paragraph removes it.
8. Locale-aware formatting and live date `Text` (`Text(_:format:)`, `Text(date, style:)`, `Text(timerInterval:)`): numbers and dates in labels are wrong in every non-English locale without it; no formatting crate is in the tree. Size M; Blocker none.

Not in the top 8: `TextRenderer` and `Text.Layout` (L; the engine exposes glyph runs, Freya's `Label` does not, so it needs a kit-owned `ElementExt`), and the macOS 26 rich `TextEditor` with `AttributedString` (L).

### 2.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified
- Misfiled input: none in this file. The hint says `FileDialog*` types were filed here; `grep -in filedialog A_text.txt` → 0. They are in `C_nav.txt` (`FileDialogBrowserOptions` and the `fileDialog*` modifiers), so this study did not classify them. `dynamicTypeSize` (modifier and environment value) is in this file; per the hint this study left it unclassified for the accessibility agent. The 7 `accessibility*` and `speech*` members of `Text` are listed as not classified here for the same reason. `SearchUnavailableContent` and `ContentUnavailableView.search` belong with the containers area; this study classified the name here.
- Disagree with matrix §7 `findNavigator` / `TextEditor` find bar (`implemented`): it is `partial` (no `isPresented` binding, no replace, no match options, `TextEditor` only).
- Disagree with matrix §7 `writingToolsBehavior, spelling, substitutions` (`partial`): `writingToolsBehavior`, `writingToolsAffordanceVisibility` and `WritingToolsBehavior` are `n/a` (Apple Intelligence service); spelling belongs to `autocorrectionDisabled`. Suggest splitting the row.
- Add to matrix §7: the SDK deprecates `.roundedBorder` and `.squareBorder` on macOS 27 in favor of `.bordered` plus `textInputBorderShape` (`SwiftUI.swiftinterface:10036-10040`, `:27947`); the row for `.bordered` should say so.
- Matrix §18 (macOS 27) is right for this area (`TextFieldStyle.bordered`, `TextInputBorderShape`). The macOS 26 items of this area are in no matrix table: `TextEditor` with `AttributedString` and `AttributedTextSelection`, `AttributedTextFormattingDefinition`, `findNavigator` family and `FindContext`, `lineHeight`, `writingDirection`, `multilineTextAlignment(strategy:)`, `Text(Image)`, `searchSelection`, `searchToolbarBehavior`, `textInputFormattingControlVisibility`. Matrix §7 mentions only the first and the `findNavigator`.
- Proposed rows (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `textSelection(.enabled)` | `NSTextField.isSelectable` | none | missing | P1
  - `Text + Text`, Markdown, `Text(Image)` | `NSAttributedString` in `NSTextField.attributedStringValue` | none | missing | P1
  - SwiftUI-named text modifiers (`bold`, `kerning`, `tracking`, `underline` ...) | `NSFont`, `NSParagraphStyle`, `NSAttributedString.Key` | Freya `TextStyleExt` names | partial | P2
  - `lineLimit(range)`, `truncationMode`, `minimumScaleFactor`, `textCase` | `NSLineBreakMode`, `maximumNumberOfLines`, `allowsDefaultTighteningForTruncation` | `Text::line_limit` | partial | P2
  - `TextSelection` binding | `NSTextView.selectedRange` | none | missing | P2
  - `textInputSuggestions`, `textInputCompletion` (macOS 15) | `NSControlTextEditingDelegate` completions | none | missing | P2
  - `onSubmit`, `submitScope` | `NSControl.action`, `NSTextFieldDelegate` | `TextField::on_submit` | partial | P2
  - `Text(_:format:)`, `Text(date, style:)`, `Text(timerInterval:)` | `Formatter`, `NSDateFormatter` | none | missing | P2
  - Bidi and `writingDirection` | `NSWritingDirection`, `baseWritingDirection` | none | missing | P2
  - `textContentType` | `NSTextContentType` | `platform/linux/ime.rs` role mapping | missing | P3
  - `TextRenderer`, `Text.Layout` | TextKit 2 `NSTextLayoutFragment` | none | missing | P3
  - `fontDesign`, `Font.system(size:weight:design:)` builder | `NSFontDescriptor.withDesign` | `ctdesign::Font` | partial | P2 (matrix §7 has a P3 `Font.system` row; it lacks italic, width, small caps and monospaced digits)
- Discrepancies in the kit itself: control labels are `&'static str` (`TextField::new`, `SearchField::new`, `TextEditor::new`, `ComboBox::new`, `ToolbarSearch::new`), so dynamic text needs `i18n::intern`, which leaks (`i18n.rs:32`). `SecureField::new` takes `State<PasswordField>` (zeroizing), not `String`, on purpose. `TextField` and `TextEditor` take a placeholder; SwiftUI `TextEditor` has none. `ctdesign::CAPTION` is 11/14 500 while the matrix maps `.caption` to `FOOTNOTE` 10/13; the glossary should say which one `Font.caption` is.
- Text styles: `Font.title2` is still 15/20 600 in `tokens.json:587` (`TITLE_2`); matrix §7 and backlog B1 already list it, P1.
- Unverified: (a) whether Skia's embedded ICU gives correct bidi shaping and line breaking in the kit's Vulkan build (the feature is on by default in `freya-skia-safe-0.104.0/Cargo.toml:89`; this study did not run anything); (b) whether the Skia decoration color defaults to the text color when `set_decoration_color` is never called; (c) what `.automatic` text field, `.bordered` and the SwiftUI `TextEditor` draw on macOS 27 (matrix: not probed); (d) whether `freya-edit` moves the caret correctly in right-to-left text; (e) whether `searchPresentationToolbarBehavior` has any effect on macOS (classified `n/a`, iOS navigation bar behavior); (f) the Freya inline-child placeholders in `paragraph()` render correctly with the kit's text cache (the kit does not use them yet); (g) the depth of Fluent's `NUMBER` built-in.

## 3. Containers, lists, tables, forms and layout

Types (160): 21 implemented, 40 partial, 78 absent, 21 n/a, 0 out of scope. View modifiers (62): 9 implemented, 15 partial, 37 absent, 1 n/a. Static style members (19): 11 implemented, 2 partial, 6 absent. Environment values (14): 6 partial, 8 absent. The 17 macOS 27.1 items (8 types, 4 modifiers, 3 style members, 2 environment values) are all absent; of the 8 macOS 26 items 3 are partial (`ScrollEdgeEffectStyle`, `scrollEdgeEffectStyle`, `scrollEdgeEffectHidden`), 3 absent (`SpacerSizing`, `labelIconToTitleSpacing`, `labelReservedIconWidth`) and 2 n/a (`DebugReplaceableView`, `TupleContent`).
The gaps come in three blocks. (1) The layout engine: no `Layout` protocol, `Grid`, alignment guides or baselines, `ViewThatFits`, `Spacer`, safe areas, `layoutPriority`. (2) The scroll API: one axis, offset-only controller, no targets, geometry, phases or bounce. (3) Containers hold text, not views: `List` and `Table` take `Vec<String>`, and the SwiftUI style protocols are data structs with no `makeBody`.
Freya 0.5.3 and Torin give for free: one-pass flex layout (`Direction`, six `Alignment` values per axis, `Size::Fill/Percentage/Flex/Fn`, min and max sizes, per-side padding and margin, `spacing`, `Content::Fit/Flex/Wrap`, `Position::Absolute/Global`), `layer` (a `zIndex`), `on_sized`/`on_visible` events, a `VirtualScrollView` for rows of known size, a `ScrollController` with fling, and a public `ElementExt::post_measure` hook that can move or hide children after the one layout pass. Torin has no grid, no baseline, no flex-shrink and no re-measure of a child with another proposal (`torin-0.5.3/src/measure.rs`, `values/alignment.rs`).

No name is merged with a sibling: each appears once under its own SwiftUI name. Notes on the `implemented` rows: `AlternatingRowBackgroundBehavior` and `alternatingRowBackgrounds` map to `TableStyle::striped` (`styles/table.rs:232`); `List` styles map to `TableStyle::inset_list`, `plain_list`, `bordered_list` (glossary line 342), `FormStyle::columns` (`styles/form.rs:84`), `HSplitView`/`VSplitView` to `SplitPanes`, `AnyView` to Freya `Element` (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/element.rs:260`), `EquatableView` to a `Component` whose props implement `PartialEq` (`element.rs:247`), `ForEach` to `ChildrenExt::children` with `KeyExt::key`, `View` to `Component` and `IntoElement`, `zIndex` to `LayerExt::layer` (`scroll.rs` uses `.layer(THUMB_LAYER)`), `padding` to `ContainerExt::padding(Gaps)`, `horizontalRadioGroupLayout` to `RadioGroupStyle::axis` (`styles/radio_group.rs:47`). The 21 `n/a` types are Apple platform types (`CarouselListStyle`, `EllipticalListStyle`: watchOS; `GroupedListStyle`: iOS and tvOS; `InsetGroupedListStyle`: iOS; `ListSectionSpacing`: iOS and watchOS) and Swift result-builder or generic wrapper machinery (`ViewBuilder`, `ContentBuilder`, `EmptyView`, `EmptyContent`, `EmptyModifier`, `ModifiedContent`, `TupleView`, `TupleContent`, `DebugReplaceableView`, `IndexedIdentifierCollection`, the five table builder and tuple types) for which Freya's `ChildrenExt::child`, `children` and `maybe_child` do the job; `scrollDismissesKeyboard` is n/a (software keyboard).

### 3.3 Area priorities

1. `List` and `Table` with view cells and rows: every real app list (mail, files, settings with controls) needs avatars, badges and controls in rows; today they hold `String`s. Size M; Blocker none (fixed or by-index row height).
2. `Layout` protocol with `AnyLayout`, `HStackLayout` and friends, and `ViewThatFits`: no way to write an adaptive or custom container; the Torin single pass limits a faithful version. Size L (placement-only M); Blocker Freya.
3. `Grid`, `GridRow`, `GridItem` with `LazyVGrid`/`LazyHGrid`: aligned forms, galleries and key grids; a P2 gap in the matrix. Size M; Blocker none.
4. `Spacer` plus `frame(alignment:)`, `fixedSize`, `layoutPriority`: the first things a SwiftUI designer writes; Torin has `Size::flex` but no shrink or ideal size. Size S for `Spacer` and `frame`, M with priorities; Blocker Freya (shrink).
5. Alignment guides and text baselines (`firstTextBaseline`, `alignmentGuide`): needed to line up mixed-size text and icons in rows. Size L; Blocker Freya (Torin alignment).
6. Safe areas and `contentMargins` (`safeAreaInset`, `ignoresSafeArea`, `ScrollView` under the macOS 27 toolbar, `ReservedRegion`): content under translucent bars is the macOS 26 and 27 look. Size M; Blocker measurement.
7. Scroll API: two axes, `scrollPosition`/`ScrollViewReader` by id, `onScrollGeometryChange`, `scrollTargetBehavior`, bounce: the controller moves by offset only. Size M; Blocker none (bounce: measurement).
8. Style protocols with `makeBody` (`GroupBoxStyle`, `FormStyle`, `ListStyle`, `TableStyle`, `DisclosureGroupStyle`, `LabeledContentStyle`) and the macOS 27.1 `ArrangementView` family: custom component looks and the new split and overlay container. Size M each; Blocker none (`ArrangementView`: measurement).

### 3.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified

- Rows this study disagrees with, all in matrix §12: `List` (`measured`, but the API takes `Vec<String>`: `partial` for view rows; backlog C.1 agrees); `LabeledContent` (`implemented` by `Row`, only inside a `Form`: `partial`); `ContentUnavailableView` (`implemented`, but no description, actions or `.search`: `partial`); `LazyVGrid`, `LazyHGrid` (`implemented` by `CollectionView`, an icon-and-name wrap grid without `GridItem`: `partial`); `ZStack`, `ViewThatFits`, `Layout` (one `partial` row: only `ZStack` has a workaround, `ViewThatFits` and `Layout` are `absent`); `Divider` (`measured`, horizontal only); `ScrollView` (`measured` for the bars, single axis only).
- Rows the matrix lacks (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri): `Spacer` | `NSStackView` gravity and `NSView.setContentHuggingPriority` | none | `missing` | P1; `frame(min/ideal/max, alignment:)`, `fixedSize`, `layoutPriority` | Auto Layout constraints, content hugging and compression | Freya `width`/`min_width` | `partial` | P1; `GeometryReader` | `NSView.frame`, `NSLayoutGuide` | `on_sized` | `partial` | P2; `ScrollViewReader`, `scrollPosition`, `defaultScrollAnchor` | `NSClipView.scroll(to:)` | `ScrollController` | `partial` | P2; `scrollTargetBehavior` | none | none | `missing` | P3; `scrollBounceBehavior` | `NSScrollView.verticalScrollElasticity` | none | `missing` | P3; `safeAreaInset`, `ignoresSafeArea`, `contentMargins` | `NSView.safeAreaInsets`, `NSScrollView.contentInsets` | `Form::pinned` | `partial` | P2; `Alignment` baselines and `alignmentGuide` | `NSLayoutConstraint.Attribute.firstBaseline` | none | `missing` | P2; `DisclosureGroup(isExpanded:)` binding | `NSButton.state` of a disclosure triangle | `DisclosureGroup::expanded` | `partial` | P2; `OutlineGroup` and table outline rows with columns | `NSOutlineView` columns | `OutlineView` (one column) | `partial` | P3; `listRow*` modifiers | `NSTableRowView` | `TableStyle` | `partial` | P3; style protocols with `makeBody` | `NSCell` subclasses | `*Style` structs | `partial` | P3; right-to-left layout | `userInterfaceLayoutDirection` | `LayoutDirection` field | `partial` | P3; `horizontalSizeClass`, `verticalSizeClass` | none | none | `missing` | P3 (candidate out of scope).
- Misfiled input items: `overlayArrangementEdge` (27.1, two overloads) is in `G_gfx.txt` but belongs to this area (it is part of `ArrangementView`; row above counts it, the classification of this file does not). `ModifiedContent` lists 27 `accessibility*` members that belong to `H_access`. `LabeledControlGroupContent` is `ControlGroup(content:label:)` and `LabeledToolbarItemGroupContent` is `ToolbarItemGroup(content:label:)` (toolbar and control area); both marked `partial` because `ControlGroup::new(label)` and `ToolGroup` exist, without a check of label views. `horizontalRadioGroupLayout` belongs to the pickers area, `scrollDismissesKeyboard` to the input area. `GeometryProxy.concentricCornerRadii` (27.0) and `containerCornerInsets` (26) belong to the shapes and graphics area.
- Naming drift (AGENTS.md, «Names and glossary»): the glossary has no row for `Spacer`, `ZStack`, `EdgeInsets` (the kit says `Gaps` from Torin), `AnyView` (Freya `Element`), `ForEach` or `Group`; if the kit adds them, the first SwiftUI name applies.
- Unverified: `TableStyle.bordered` and `.automatic` are `implemented` on the word of matrix §12 (bench `tables`, mapped to `TableStyle::full_width`, `plain`); this study did not rerun the bench. The semantics of `fixedSize` against `Size::Inner` (whether text still wraps to the parent's available width); whether the macOS 27 `.automatic` `FormStyle` is `.columns` as from memory it (no probe; the kit default is the grouped card); whether `on_visible` clips at a `ScrollView` viewport in the kit's wrapper (the doc says "clipping ancestors"); the label support of `ControlGroup`; the exact behavior of `ReservedRegion` kinds (`occlusion`, `division`), read from the interface only; Wayland and compositor side effects of safe areas.

## 4. Navigation, tabs, toolbar and presentation

The closed list has 104 types, 87 view modifiers, 17 static style members, 11 environment values and 46 members added to the area's types (265 names). Types: 2 implemented, 26 partial, 46 absent, 30 n/a. Modifiers: 4 implemented, 27 partial, 34 absent, 21 n/a, 1 out of scope. Style members: 2 implemented, 5 partial, 8 absent, 2 n/a. Environment values: 0 implemented, 1 partial, 9 absent, 1 n/a. Members: 0 implemented, 3 partial, 30 absent, 13 n/a. The gaps are the declarative models (a toolbar built from `ToolbarItem`s, a typed `NavigationStack`, `Tab(value:)`) and the binding-driven presentations (`isPresented`, `item`, `onDismiss`); the kit draws the looks but exposes imperative or presence-driven pieces. Many macOS 26 and 27 additions (`ToolbarMinimizationBehavior`, `ToolbarItemAxisBehavior`, `ToolbarVerticalCompressionBehavior`, `PresentationPlacement`, `TabSectionExpansion`, `TabBarMinimizeBehavior`) expose only `.automatic` on macOS, so they are `n/a`. Freya gives layers, `on_sized` and overlay positioning; it gives no router in the pinned set (`freya-components-0.5.3/Cargo.toml`: feature `router = ["dep:freya-router"]`, no `freya-router` in the registry, and ADR 0033 builds `freya-components` with no default features).

### 4.1 Gaps and discrepancies

#### Navigation detail: what the kit can express today

With `History<T>`, `NavigationButtons` and `Row::page`, an app can:

- Keep a linear list of any `T: Clone + PartialEq` pages with `push`, `back`, `forward`, `current`, `can_go_back` and `can_go_forward`. `push` drops the forward entries and ignores a repeat of the current page (`views/navigation.rs`, two unit tests).
- Draw the back and forward chevrons in a `ToolGroup`, wired to Alt+Left and Alt+Right (`GO_BACK`, `GO_FORWARD`).
- Open a pane of a `SettingsWindow` from a row (`Row::page(title, pane_id)`) or from code (`SettingsWindow::navigation_destination(State<Option<String>>)`). Both work only inside a `SettingsWindow` (`PageLink` context) and only by a `&'static str` id.
- Switch the detail of a `SplitView` by keeping the selection in app state and matching on it.

What it cannot express, compared with `NavigationStack(path:)`, `navigationDestination(for:)` and `NavigationLink(value:)`:

1. A stack. `History` is Safari-like: back keeps a forward list. A `NavigationStack` path only pops; the path has `append`, `removeLast(k)`, `removeAll`, `count`, and the app edits it directly. The kit has no `pop_to_root`, no truncate, no length and no way to bind the stack to app state (`History` is owned by a `State<History<T>>`, edits go through its methods).
2. Typed destinations. SwiftUI registers `navigationDestination(for: Recipe.self)` once and any `NavigationLink(value: recipe)` below it resolves. The kit has no registry and no link view outside the settings pane id; every app writes its own `match` on the current route.
3. A link view. `NavigationLink` is a row or button that pushes on press, shows the disclosure chevron (`navigationLinkIndicatorVisibility`), and is also the selection source in a `List(selection:)`. The kit has `Row::page` only.
4. Toolbar and title integration. A pushed destination shows a back chevron in the titlebar and its own `navigationTitle`, subtitle and toolbar items; `navigationBarBackButtonHidden` hides the chevron. The kit's `Titlebar` takes one title and two slots from the app.
5. Transitions, restoration and sharing. The push and pop animation, `NavigationPath.codable` for state restoration, and `NavigationSplitView` columns that each own a stack.

A typed-path API needs, in order:

1. A route type owned by the app, in Rust one enum per stack (a `TypeId` registry only when one stack mixes unrelated types).
2. A `State<Vec<Route>>` (or a small `NavigationPath<Route>` wrapper with `push`, `pop`, `pop_to_root`, `len`, and `serde` support) that the app can read and write.
3. A `NavigationStack` view that renders the root, then the last route through the destination closure, and provides the path through context.
4. A `NavigationLink` view and a `use_navigation_path::<Route>()` hook so deep children push without a callback chain.
5. A titlebar hook: the stack publishes "can go back" and the destination's title; `Titlebar` draws one back chevron (`NavigationButtons` keeps the forward button for `History`).
6. A push and pop transition from `animation.rs`, off with Reduce Motion.

`History` can stay for browser-like panes; `NavigationStack` replaces it for push and pop.
