### 4.3 Area priorities

1. `NavigationStack` with a typed path, `navigationDestination(for:)` and `NavigationLink(value:)`: every multi-screen app needs push and pop, and `History` plus `Row::page` cover only browser-style panes and settings ids. Size M; Blocker none.
2. A declarative toolbar (`toolbar`, `ToolbarItem(placement:)`, `.principal`, `ToolbarSpacer`, labels, automatic overflow): the titlebar has two slots and icon-only items, so an app cannot lay out a real macOS toolbar. Size M; Blocker none (measurement for spacers and labels).
3. Binding-driven presentation for `sheet`, `alert`, `confirmationDialog` and `popover` (`isPresented`, `item`, `onDismiss`, roles, `arrowEdge`): today the app adds and removes components by hand and gets no dismiss callback. Size M; Blocker Wayland protocol for programmatic popover grabs (`xdg_popup.grab` needs an input serial).
4. `NavigationSplitView` with a content column and a `NavigationSplitViewVisibility` binding: three-pane apps (Mail, Notes) cannot be built and the sidebar state cannot be restored. Size M; Blocker none.
5. `navigationSubtitle`, `toolbarTitleDisplayMode` and `ToolbarTitleMenu`: document apps show a subtitle and a title menu; the titlebar draws one string. Size S to M; Blocker measurement (matrix backlog D.3).
6. `TabView` with value selection, `Tab` badge and disabled, `TabSection`: settings and document apps select tabs by id, not by index, and need unread badges. Size M; Blocker none.
7. File dialogs attached to the window (`xdg-foreign-v2` parent, filters, folder, accept label) for `fileImporter`, `fileExporter`, `fileMover`: the portal call passes an empty parent, so Open and Save panels are not modal to the app window. Size M; Blocker compositor (malaysia has no `zxdg_exporter_v2`).
8. `WindowToolbarStyle` chosen by the app (`.expanded`, `.unified` with or without sidebar): `Titlebar` guesses the bar from `with_sidebar`. Size M; Blocker compositor (a new `Bar` value in `limitless_window_style_v1`).

### 4.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified

- Rows this study disagrees with:
  - §13 `.popover` is `implemented` P1. I call it `partial`: `Popover::new((w, h), content).show(Area)` is imperative, fixed size, below-only, with no `isPresented`, `item`, `arrowEdge` or `.point` anchor, and does not expose a dismiss callback.
  - §13 `.confirmationDialog` is `implemented`. I call it `partial`: there is no presentation binding, no button roles and no title visibility, and the number of actions is the `add_button` list around fixed confirm and cancel.
  - §13 `.contextMenu` is `implemented` P1. I call it `partial`: the items are `Vec<MenuItem>` with an id callback, not a view builder, and only a secondary press opens it.
  - §13 `.fileImporter`, `.fileExporter` is `partial` P3. The portal call passes no parent window, no filters and no folder, and nothing in the kit calls `portal::open_files`; P2 is closer.
  - §13 `.sheet` is `partial`: agree, and the gap is also API: no `isPresented`, `item`, `onDismiss`, `presentationSizing`.
  - §12 `TabView` `.sidebarAdaptable`, `.tabBarOnly`, `Tab`, `TabSection` is one `missing` P3 row. This study split it: `Tab(value:)` selection, badge and disabled (P2); `TabSection` and `TabRole` (P3); `.sidebarAdaptable` with `TabViewCustomization` (P3); `.grouped` (P3).
  - §12 `NavigationStack` is `partial` P2. Agree; section 1 spells out what is missing.
  - §12 `inspector(isPresented:)` is `implemented`. Agree for the pane; its width is fixed (`SplitViewStyle::inspector_width`), so `inspectorColumnWidth` is a separate gap.
  - §14 `ToolbarItem(placement:)` and `ToolbarItemGroup` are `partial`: agree. `ToolbarItemGroup` is `ToolGroup`; there is no placement enum.
  - §18 lists `ToolbarMinimizationBehavior`, `ToolbarVerticalCompressionBehavior`, `ToolbarItemAxisBehavior`, `PresentationPlacement` and `TabSectionExpansion` as gaps. In this SDK each exposes only `.automatic` on macOS (the other members are `macOS, unavailable`), so they need no kit work; `toolbarTitleAlignment` the modifier is iOS only, while the type and the environment value are macOS 27.2. `TabRole.prominent` is available on macOS (`anyAppleOS 27.0`).
- Rows the matrix lacks (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `navigationSplitViewVisibility` binding, content column | `NSSplitViewController` items | `SidebarControl`, `SplitView::sidebar_visible` | partial | P2
  - `inspectorColumnWidth` | `NSSplitViewItem.minimumThickness`, `maximumThickness` | `SplitViewStyle::inspector_width` | partial | P3
  - `toolbarTitleDisplayMode`, `ToolbarTitleMenu` | `NSWindow.titleVisibility`, title menu | none | absent | P3
  - `ToolbarItem(.principal)`, `ToolbarItem(.status)` | `NSToolbar.centeredItemIdentifier` | none | absent | P2
  - `badge(_:)` on tabs, rows, items | `NSTabViewItem`, `NSTableCellView` badge | `Badge` | partial | P2
  - `windowDismissBehavior`, `windowMinimizeBehavior`, `windowResizeBehavior`, `windowFullScreenBehavior` | `NSWindow.styleMask`, standard button `isEnabled` | `TrafficLights::zoom_supported`, `Titlebar::close_only` | partial | P2
  - `windowToolbarFullScreenVisibility` | `NSWindow.toolbar` full-screen behavior | `WindowToolbarFullScreenVisibility` | implemented | P3
  - `dialogPreventsAppTermination`, `presentationPreventsAppTermination` | `applicationShouldTerminate` | none | absent | P3
  - `dismissalConfirmationDialog` | `windowShouldClose` | none | absent | P3
  - `refreshable`, `renameAction`, `sectionActions` | none | none | absent | P3
  - `safeAreaBar` | `NSTitlebarAccessoryViewController`, `NSScrollPocket` | `TopGlass` | partial | P2
  - `isSearching`, `dismissSearch` | `NSSearchToolbarItem` | `ToolbarSearch` | partial | P3
- Misfiled input items (classified here, belong elsewhere): `DefaultPickerStyle` (pickers, A1); `ScrollDismissesKeyboardMode` (input, E); `asyncImageURLSession` (graphics and networking, G; this study classes it `out of scope` under ADR 0083); `SidebarListStyle` and `SidebarRowSize` (lists, B); `DismissSearchAction`, `isSearching`, `dismissSearch`, `searchSuggestionsPlacement` (search); `windowResizeAnchor`, `windowDismissBehavior`, `windowMinimizeBehavior`, `windowResizeBehavior`, `windowFullScreenBehavior`, `presentedWindowStyle` (scenes, D); `assistiveAccessNavigationIcon` (accessibility, H). The hint names `navigationTransition`, but it and its types are in the G input (`AutomaticNavigationTransition` is the only macOS one; `ZoomNavigationTransition` and `CrossFadeNavigationTransition` are macOS-unavailable); this study did not classify them.
- Classified `n/a` without a macOS probe (unverified): `presentationDetents`, `presentationDragIndicator`, `presentationContentInteraction`, `presentationBackgroundInteraction`, `presentationCompactAdaptation`, `PresentationDetent`, `CustomPresentationDetent`, `PresentationAdaptation` (the SDK marks them macOS 13; matrix §13 says detents are iOS), `persistentSystemOverlays`, `sectionIndexLabel`, `assistiveAccessNavigationIcon`, `scrollDismissesKeyboard`, and `NavigationSplitViewStyle.balanced` and `.prominentDetail` (documented as iPad column behavior). If a probe shows an effect on a Mac window, move them to `absent`. The `fileDialog*` modifiers marked `n/a` (`fileDialogBrowserOptions`, `fileDialogCustomizationID`, `fileDialogImportsUnresolvedAliases`, `fileDialogURLEnabled`, `fileExporterFilenameLabel`) have no counterpart in the FileChooser portal as far as known; the portal option list is from memory of the xdg-desktop-portal docs and was not re-fetched.
- Wayland claims verified in the pinned crates: `xdg_toplevel.set_parent` (`wayland-protocols-0.32.13/protocols/stable/xdg-shell/xdg-shell.xml:663`), `xdg_popup.grab` (:1290), `xdg_popup.reposition` (:1368, since 3), `xdg_dialog_v1.set_modal` (`staging/xdg-dialog/xdg-dialog-v1.xml:87`), `zxdg_exporter_v2.export_toplevel` (`unstable/xdg-foreign/xdg-foreign-unstable-v2.xml:80`). Not verified: where malaysia places a modal dialog (the kit comment says centred), and whether any compositor honors the Inhibit portal's logout flag for `dialogPreventsAppTermination`.
- `docs/barrierkit/18-platform.md` "Wayland protocols" table does not list `xdg_wm_dialog_v1`, which `platform/linux/window.rs` (`fn create_window`) binds for sheets and `platform/linux/runtime.rs` (`dialog_manager`) stores.

## 5. App, scenes, windows, commands and documents

Types (153): 1 implemented, 34 partial, 46 absent, 72 n/a. Scene modifiers (31; the input's view-modifier section is empty): 3 implemented, 19 partial, 5 absent, 4 n/a. Static style members (10): 0 implemented, 6 partial, 1 absent, 3 n/a. Environment values (23): 2 implemented, 6 partial, 8 absent, 7 n/a. The gaps are the scene model behind `Application::new(..).window(..).run()` (one window, a flat per-window `MainMenu`, a status icon tied to that window), the document model (26 types, 12 new in macOS 27) and app lifecycle hooks; 72 types are `n/a` (visionOS, WidgetKit, AppKit hosting, Apple-only scenes). Freya gives contexts (`provide_context`, `use_consume`), effects (`use_side_effect`) and custom elements (`ElementExt`, used by `canvas::Canvas`), but no window or scene concept, so all window behaviour is the kit's own runner in `crates/bk/src/platform/`. No ADR in `docs/decisions.md` excludes anything here (ADR 0047: "nothing is built until an app needs it"), so no row uses `out of scope`. macOS 26 news here is `n/a` (`NSHostingSceneRepresentation`, `AssistiveAccess`, `ControlWidget*`, `RemoteImmersiveSpace`); macOS 27 brings the `Document` model and deprecates `FileDocument` and `ReferenceFileDocument` ("Conform your type to Document instead").


### 5.1 Gaps and discrepancies

| SwiftUI API (family) | Since | Status | Kit today | Used for | Rust/Freya way | Size | Blocker |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `WindowGroup(id:for:)`, `Window(id:)`, `OpenWindowAction` (`openWindow`), `DismissWindowAction` (`dismissWindow`), `PresentedWindowContent` | | implemented (was partial): `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `use_open_window`, `use_dismiss_window` (`crates/bk/src/app/windows.rs`, [Scenes](../barrierkit/08-windows.md#scenes), recipe `scenes.rs`) | `Application::window(title, content)` gives one window. `WindowControls::open_window(AppComponent, WindowOptions)` (`platform/window.rs:317`, handled at `:680`) opens another main window; `UtilityWindow::open()` opens a panel; `WindowControls::close()` closes only the caller's own window. No ids, no value-based windows, no "front the window if already open", no dismiss of another window. `grep -rniE 'window_?group\|dismiss_?window' crates` → 0. | An editor window per item, reopen a closed window, inspector windows, restore each window with its value | `Application::window_group(id, content)` and an `OpenWindow` handle in a root context next to `WindowControls`: `open(id)`, `open_value(id, value)` with `Hash + Serialize + DeserializeOwned`, `dismiss(id)`; the runner already creates windows on `WindowCommand::Open` | M | none |

A main window, a Settings window, a status icon and commands cannot be declared together. | The app declaration an app designer reads first: windows, settings, menu bar item, commands in one place | `Application::scene(..)` chain taking `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `Settings`, `MenuBarExtra` values and `Commands`; rename `app::Scene` when the second scene type lands (glossary rule 3, name the old one there). `SceneBuilder` is a Swift result builder: `n/a`, `Vec` and method chains (ADR 0061) replace it | M | none |
| `Commands`, `CommandMenu`, `CommandGroup`, `CommandGroupPlacement` (21 placements), `.commands`, `.commandsRemoved`, `.commandsReplaced`, `CommandsBuilder` | | partial: `Commands`, `CommandGroup`, `CommandGroupPlacement` exist (`crates/bk/src/app/commands.rs`, `bk::prelude`, [Standard menus](../barrierkit/13-app.md#standard-menus)); the standard menus are opt-in | `MainMenu::new(vec![Menu::new(title, items)])` passed per window to `WindowOptions::menu`, exported over dbusmenu and `org_kde_kwin_appmenu_manager` (`platform/main_menu_export.rs`). No name was merged with a sibling: every `Foo` and `FooConfiguration` appears on its own.


### 5.3 Area priorities

1. Multi-scene `App` (`WindowGroup(id:for:)`, `Window`, `openWindow`, `dismissWindow`): a one-window `Application` cannot express most real apps (editors, inspectors, per-item windows). The runner already opens windows; the registry and the id and value handle are missing. Size M; Blocker none.
2. `Commands`, `CommandGroup`, `CommandGroupPlacement` and the standard menus: every Mac app has App, File, Edit, View, Window and Help; the kit makes the app write them from nothing and has no Quit or Close Window action. Size M; Blocker measurement.
3. `Settings` scene with `openSettings` and `SettingsLink`: `SettingsWindow` exists as content but nothing opens it the macOS way. Size S; Blocker none (needs row 1).
4. `MenuBarExtra` completeness: pixmap or symbol icon, live label, menu on click, `isInserted`, and windowless apps; `.window` style later. Size M (L for `.window`); Blocker compositor for `.window`.
5. App lifecycle hooks (`NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor`): close confirmation, quit hook, open files and URLs, keep alive. The kit closes on the compositor's request and exits with the last window. Size S; Blocker none.
6. `DocumentGroup` and the macOS 27 `Document` model: the base of every file-based app; large but self-contained, builds on rows 1, 2 and 5. Size L; Blocker none (design).
7. `windowResizability(.contentSize)` and `windowStyle(.hiddenTitleBar, .plain)`: small, visible on first launch. Size S; Blocker measurement for the hidden-title-bar corner.
8. `NSViewRepresentable` equivalent (foreign surface in a view): needed for web, video and terminal content. Size L; Blocker Freya.


### 5.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified

- Matrix rows this study disagrees with:
  - §13 `Commands`, `CommandMenu` = `implemented`: `partial`. Only a flat per-window `MainMenu`; no `CommandGroupPlacement`, no standard menus, no scene-level commands.
  - §13 `MenuBarExtra` = `implemented`: `partial`. Icon by name only, `ItemIsMenu` false, tied to a window, no `.window` style, no windowless app.
  - §14 `WindowGroup`, `Window`, `Settings` = `implemented`: `partial`. One window per `Application`; no id, value, `Settings` scene or `openSettings`.
  - §14 `DocumentGroup`, `AlertScene` = `missing`: split them. `AlertScene` is `partial` (`Alert` exists as a child sheet).
  - §14 `UtilityWindow` = `implemented`: agree (`app::UtilityWindow`, `PanelBehavior::UTILITY`); it has no id, so `openWindow(id:)` cannot reach it.
  - §18 lists `Document`, `DocumentReader`, `DocumentWriter` only: macOS 27 has 12 document types, and `FileDocument` and `ReferenceFileDocument` are deprecated in 27.
- Rows the matrix lacks (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `openWindow`, `dismissWindow`, `WindowGroup(id:for:)` | `NSWindowController` | `WindowControls::open_window` | partial | P1
  - `Commands` standard menus, `CommandGroupPlacement` | `NSApplication.mainMenu` | `MainMenu` | partial | P1
  - `SidebarCommands`, `TextEditingCommands`, `TextFormattingCommands`, `ToolbarCommands`, `InspectorCommands` | `NSResponder` chain | `TOGGLE_SIDEBAR` | partial | P2
  - `openSettings`, `Settings` scene | `NSApp.sendAction(showSettingsWindow:)` | `SettingsWindow` | partial | P1
  - `MenuBarExtra` `.window` | `NSPopover` on `NSStatusItem` | none | absent | P2
  - `NSViewRepresentable` | `NSView` hosting | `canvas::Canvas` (paint only) | absent | P3
  - `NSApplicationDelegateAdaptor`, close interception | `NSApplicationDelegate`, `windowShouldClose` | `Instance` | absent | P2
  - `windowResizability`, `windowIdealSize` | `NSWindow.contentMinSize`, `zoom` | `WindowOptions::min_size` | partial | P2
  - `windowLevel`, `windowManagerRole` | `NSWindow.level`, `collectionBehavior` | `PanelBehavior` | partial | P3
  - `scenePhase`, `appearsActive` | `NSApplication.isActive`, `NSWindow.isKeyWindow` | `WindowStatus::activated` | partial | P2
  - `SceneStorage`, `AppStorage` | `NSWindowRestoration`, `UserDefaults` | `load_restoration`, `use_defaults` | partial | P2
- Misfiled input items (still classified): `AppStorage`, `SceneStorage`, `ScenePhase`, `defaultAppStorage` (state), `PreviewPlatform` (previews), `MoveCommandDirection` (input), `Alignment3D`, `DepthAlignment*`, `ViewDimensions3D`, `Viewpoint3D`, `SquareAzimuth`, `OrnamentAttachmentAnchor` (spatial layout), `DeviceHinge*` (foldable hardware), `onChange` (state), `refresh`, `rename`, `undoManager`, `managedObjectContext` (state and data), `dismiss`, `isPresented`, `presentationMode` (presentation). The `VIEW MODIFIERS` section of the input is empty: the list holds 31 scene modifiers, so the line `view modifiers` below counts those. The `windowDismissBehavior`, `windowMinimizeBehavior`, `windowResizeBehavior`, `windowFullScreenBehavior` view modifiers use `WindowInteractionBehavior` and are filed elsewhere: they map to `TrafficLights::close_only`, `zoom_supported` and `WindowStatus::fixed` (partial).
- Wayland protocol status for the window features (pinned `wayland-protocols` 0.32.13 with `client`, `staging`, `unstable`, `Cargo.toml:69`; smithay-client-toolkit 0.20.0):
  - Used: `xdg-decoration` (`WindowDecorations::RequestClient`, `platform/linux/window.rs` `fn create_window`; a server-side answer turns off edge resizing, ADR 0035), `xdg-dialog` (`set_modal` for sheets, `fn create_window`), `xdg-activation` (`platform/linux/activation.rs`), `xdg_toplevel.set_parent` (utility windows and sheets), `org_kde_kwin_appmenu`, `limitless_window_style_v1` (Malaysia only).
  - In the pinned crates, unused by the kit (`grep -rniE` on each name in `crates` → 0): `xdg-toplevel-icon` (window and document icons), `xdg-foreign` v2 (parent for portal dialogs), `xdg-session-management` (restore position and state), `xdg-toplevel-tag`, `xdg-toplevel-drag`, `wl_subcompositor` (sctk `subcompositor.rs`).
  - Cannot be done by a client: toplevel position (`defaultPosition`, the position half of `WindowPlacement`), always-on-top level, app-wide window list. The compositor (malaysia) or a private protocol must offer them.
- Naming: `app::Scene` means one window and clashes with SwiftUI's `Scene` protocol; `app::Application` is the `App`. If scene types are added, rename `Scene` (glossary rule 3, record the old name).
- Discrepancy to note in the docs: the process exits when the last main window closes (`platform/linux/window.rs` `fn close_window`); SwiftUI apps keep running. Shortcuts are Ctrl based, not Command (`Shortcut::ctrl`).
- Unverified: the exact standard menu items per `CommandGroupPlacement` (needs a macOS probe); the corner radius and lights of a window with no bar (`Bar` has no such kind; this study did not read `chrome.rs`); whether Malaysia serves `xdg-foreign`, `xdg-session-management` and `xdg-toplevel-icon` (compositor not read); how KDE and GNOME hosts treat `ItemIsMenu` and `Activate(x, y)` of the StatusNotifierItem spec; Freya's `ElementExt` could not paint an external dmabuf (this study read only `canvas.rs`, not the Freya renderer).

## 6. Input: gestures, focus, shortcuts, drag and drop, pointer

Counts (closed list of 182 names, plus 40 members): types 96 (2 implemented, 17 partial, 42 absent, 35 n/a), view modifiers 74 (1 implemented, 20 partial, 38 absent, 15 n/a), static style members 4 (0 implemented, 2 partial, 2 absent), environment values 8 (0 implemented, 1 partial, 5 absent, 2 n/a), and 40 members added to the area's types (0 implemented, 6 partial, 14 absent, 20 n/a, mostly Swift `hash`/`description` plumbing and the `Gesture` combinators). Nothing is `out of scope`: no ADR in `docs/decisions.md` covers input (`grep -niE 'gesture|drag|drop|focus|keyboard|shortcut|hover|pointer' docs/decisions.md` hits only the traffic-light, sidebar and `focus_ring` lines). The gaps are one big block (a `Gesture` API with composition, arena and recognizers), a drag-and-drop layer limited to files, and a thin focus model (no `FocusState`, no `FocusedValue`). Freya gives raw events for free: mouse, touch (with `finger_id`, `phase`, `force`), wheel with `WheelSource::{Line,Pixel}` and `TouchPhase`, key down/up with modifiers, focus by `AccessibilityId` with Tab/Shift+Tab/Up/Down traversal, `.cursor(CursorIcon)` and `.cursor_visible(bool)`, `interactive(bool)`, and a file-drop event. It gives no gesture recognition: `ragnarok-0.5.3` only does hit testing, hover/press state and propagation (`grep -rniE 'gesture|pinch|rotate|long|double' ragnarok-0.5.3/src` finds only the word "longer" in two comments). Wayland side: the kit binds `wl_seat` (pointer, keyboard, touch), `wp_cursor_shape_v1`, `wl_data_device_manager`, primary selection and `text_input_v3`; it does not bind `zwp_pointer_gestures_v1` (swipe, pinch, hold), `wp_tablet_v2`, `zwp_relative_pointer`, `zwp_pointer_constraints` or `xdg_toplevel_drag`, although `wayland-protocols-0.32.13` carries all of them (`wp::pointer_gestures::zv1` under the `unstable` feature that the workspace enables, `Cargo.toml:69`) and `smithay-client-toolkit-0.20.0` carries only the constraints and relative-pointer helpers.

### 6.3 Area priorities
1. `Gesture` protocol with `.gesture`, `.highPriorityGesture`, `.simultaneousGesture` and the combinators: every custom interaction in a real app is written as a gesture; today each control hand-rolls pointer tracking and one element holds one handler per event. Size L; Blocker Freya (`element.rs:481`, no capture phase for press).
2. `.keyboardShortcut` with `.defaultAction` and `.cancelAction`: shortcuts work only through menu items, so a dialog or toolbar button cannot own Return, Escape or a key chord. Size S; Blocker none.
3. Typed drag and drop (`onDrop`, `dropDestination`, `draggable`, `DropDelegate`, `isTargeted`): drops are files only and drags carry files or text, so text or items cannot be dropped on fields, lists or between views. Size M; Blocker Freya (file-only drop event), the kit can route around it.
4. `reorderContainer` (27) with the older move, delete and `swipeActions` row editing: no list, table or grid can reorder or swipe. Size M each; Blocker measurement.
5. `FocusState` with `.focused(_:equals:)` and `defaultFocus`: apps cannot move focus between named fields or choose the initial focus. Size S; Blocker none.
6. `FocusedValue` and `focusedSceneValue`: menu commands cannot act on the focused view or document. Size M; Blocker Freya (no focus-within query).
7. `onTapGesture(count:)` and `LongPressGesture`: no double click or long press outside the titlebar. Size S; Blocker none.
8. `MagnifyGesture` and `RotateGesture`: no touchpad zoom or rotate for canvases and images. Size L; Blocker compositor (and the unbound `zwp_pointer_gestures_v1`).

### 6.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified
- Matrix §13, row `.draggable`, `.dropDestination`: it says "drop only on the image well". The kit takes file drops on any element (`13-app.md` "Drag and drop", Freya `on_file_drop`); `ImageWell` is one consumer. Drag out carries `Files` and `Text`. Propose: `partial`: files in, files and text out, Copy only, no typed payload, no drag image; priority P2 (typed drops decide real apps), not P3.
- Matrix §17, row "focus ring, full keyboard access": agree with `partial`; add that `ViewModifiers::focus_ring` only draws a border and the focus system is `Interaction` plus Freya `use_focus`; add the Tab-stop rule (every `a11y_focusable` control is a stop; macOS needs Keyboard navigation) as the actual gap.
- Matrix §12 and §18 list `reorderable()`, `reorderContainer`, `reorderDestination` as one row: `reorderable()` is on `DynamicViewContent` (`SwiftUI.swiftinterface:13125`), not in this area's input. `swipeActionsContainer` (27) is missing from §18.
- Rows the matrix lacks (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `Gesture`, `.gesture`, `.highPriorityGesture`, `.simultaneousGesture` | `NSGestureRecognizer` | none | `missing` | P1

  - `MagnifyGesture`, `RotateGesture` | `NSMagnificationGestureRecognizer`, `NSRotationGestureRecognizer` | none | `missing` | P2

  - `exportableToServices`, `importableFromServices` | `NSServicesProvider` | none | `n/a` (Apple Services menu) | -
  - Pencil (9 names), `HoverEffect` family (12 names), `DigitalCrown*`, `EditMode`, `TextInputDictation*`, `UIGestureRecognizerRepresentable*`, `NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable*` (macOS 26, AppKit interop, no `NSView` exists) | n/a
- Misfiled items (belong to another area, still classified here): `invalidatableContent` (WidgetKit redaction, n/a), `userActivity`, `onContinueUserActivity`, `interactionActivityTrackingTag` (Handoff and Apple analytics, n/a); `ScrollInputBehavior`, `ScrollInputKind` (scroll; the macOS-available members are only watchOS and visionOS inputs, so n/a); `InterfaceOrientation` (windows or scenes); `allowsWindowActivationEvents`, `onInteractiveResizeChange`, `FrameResizePosition`, `FrameResizeDirection` (windows); `buttonRepeatBehavior`, `ButtonRepeatBehavior`, `PasteButton` (buttons); `EditActions`, `EditableCollectionContent`, `deleteDisabled`, `moveDisabled`, `selectionDisabled`, `swipeActions*`, `typeSelectEquivalent` (lists and collections); `ItemProviderTableRowModifier`, `OnInsertTableRowModifier` (tables); `TextInputDictation*` (text); `CoordinateSpace*` (layout and geometry).
- The only `gesture` in the input is the `NSGestureRecognizerRepresentable` overload (macOS 26, n/a). This study classified the name `gesture` as `absent` for the `Gesture` overload (`gesture(_:including:)`, not in the input). In the same way the input has only the container overload of `draggable` (macOS 26), classified `absent`; the `Transferable` overload `draggable(_:)` is covered by the partial row `onDrag`/`draggable`. `focusable` lists only the one-argument overload; `focusable(_:interactions:)` and `FocusInteractions` are absent.
- Design note: `EventModifiers.command` has no kit counterpart by design: the kit maps primary to Control (`menu::shortcut` names `primary`, `ctrl`, `control` to Control; `cmd`, `command`, `super` to Super), and `format_shortcut` prints glyphs for those. This study counted `EventModifiers` as implemented through Freya `Modifiers` (`app::Shortcut::new`, `platform::modifier_flags`); Caps Lock and Num Lock are stripped on purpose (`input/mod.rs` test).
- Compositor facts (malaysia, read only): `handlers.rs:602` accepts DnD grabs for pointer and touch but ignores the icon; `CursorShapeManagerState` is present (`state.rs:305`); no pointer gestures. The pinned smithay revision's support for `zwp_pointer_gestures_v1` is unverified (its source is not in the local cargo registry).
- Unverified: whether Freya's `element_location` and `FileEventData.cursor` are logical or physical pixels for every event (`clipboard.rs` converts with `physical_point`); whether macOS's double-click interval and key-repeat delay can be read as settings from the kit's `ctappearance`; whether macOS passes the first click through to controls of an inactive window (SwiftUI and AppKit differ per control); libinput has no haptic output API (from memory); Tab-stop rules of macOS Full Keyboard Access (needs a probe); how Smithay-based compositors deliver `wl_data_device.drop` timing to a source that is also the target (in-app DnD works through the same path as foreign drops, not tested here).

## 7. State, data flow, environment and previews

Counts (closed list of 104 names). Types (56): 2 implemented, 23 partial, 22 absent, 5 n/a, 4 out of scope. View modifiers (34): 2 implemented, 14 partial, 14 absent, 4 n/a. Static style members: none in this area. Environment values (14): 1 implemented, 7 partial, 4 absent, 2 n/a. The gaps are not widgets. They are the shape of the data flow: Rust has no property wrappers, so `@State`, `@Binding`, `@Environment`, `@AppStorage` become Freya hooks (`use_state`, `use_consume`, `use_memo`...) and the kit's own `use_defaults`. Controls are controlled (`.value(v).on_change(f)`), so there is no `Binding` type. Freya gives for free: `State<T>` with automatic read tracking (`.read()` subscribes the running component, memo, effect or future, `freya-core-0.5.3/src/lifecycle/state.rs:306`), `Memo`, effects, scoped tasks, type-keyed contexts, `Writable<T>`, `Component: PartialEq` memoization and `.key(..)` identity. What dominates the absent list: transactions, preferences, container values, previews, an ambient `isEnabled`, and time zone, calendar and locale values.

### 7.1 Gaps and discrepancies
| SwiftUI API (family) | Since | Status | Kit today | Used for | Rust/Freya way | Size | Blocker |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `Binding`, `Bindable`, `$model.field`, Binding collection members (`index`, `indices`, `id`, `startIndex`, `endIndex`, `formIndex`), `Binding.animation`, `Binding.transaction` | | partial | No `Binding` type. Controls take a value and a callback: `Switch::new(..).on(*wifi.read()).on_toggle(move \|on\| wifi.set(on))` (`crates/bk/examples/toggle.rs`, hard rule 4 of `.claude/skills/barrierkit-app/SKILL.md`). Freya has `Writable<T>` (`freya-core/src/lifecycle/writable.rs:53`, `map` at :162 gives a sub-field binding) and `WritableSetter<T>` (`writable_setter.rs:13`, value plus `on_change`), but no kit control accepts them: `grep -rnE 'Writable\|WritableSetter\|IntoWritable' crates agents docs/barrierkit` finds only `WritableUtils` imports. | Two-way link of a control to a model field, passing a slice of state to a subview, `ForEach($items)` | A re-export `Binding<T>` = Freya `Writable<T>`; each control gets `.binding(w)` that sets `.value` and `.on_change` from it; `Writable::map` for `$model.field`; a `Binding::constant(v)` for a fixed value | M | none |
| `Observable`, `@Observable` (outside the interfaces), `ObservableObject`/`@Published` (Combine, outside the interfaces), `ObservedObject`, `StateObject`, `EnvironmentObject` | | partial | `grep -rniE 'Observable\|ObservableObject\|Published\b' crates` finds only `published` locals and `app/published.rs`. The model is `State<T>`: it is `Copy` and any `.write()` notifies every reader of that whole `State`. Not documented in `agents/` as a pattern. | Model objects shared by views, a view owning its model, per-property invalidation | A struct whose fields are each a `State<_>`, built once with `use_hook` and passed by value (it is `Copy` if the fields are). That gives `@Observable` per-property tracking. `Memo<T>` is the cached computed property. Needs a recipe, not code. | S | none |
| `Environment`, `EnvironmentKey`, `Entry`, `EnvironmentValues`, `environment(_:_:)`, `transformEnvironment`, `EnvironmentObject`/`environmentObject`, `EnvironmentalModifier` | | partial | Custom values work through Freya contexts: `use_provide_context(\|\| T)`, `use_consume::<T>()`, `try_consume_context`, `use_provide_root_context` (`freya-core/src/lifecycle/context.rs:243-295`). The kit uses them (9 `provide_context(` and 63 `try_consume_*` call lines; `State<Resolved>` at `theme.rs:76`, `State<Theme>` at `styles/theme.rs:130`, `ToolbarItems` at `compact.rs:26`). Differences: the key is the Rust type, not a key path; `use_provide_context` and `use_consume` run once through `use_hook`, so the value is not reactive unless it is a `State<T>` or `Memo<T>`; there is no `.environment(..)` method on a view, a wrapper `Component` must provide the context; no default value (`try_consume` returns `Option`). `StyleModifiers::control_style` and `control_size` (`modifiers.rs:62-67`) are the kit's environment modifiers, and `use_appearance` is the appearance one. `grep -rniE 'EnvironmentKey\|@Entry\|environment_key' crates docs/barrierkit agents` → 0. | Custom values an app passes down (current document, user, feature flags), override for a subtree | A generic provider component (`Provide<T>` holding a `State<T>`, like `StyleScope` at `styles/theme.rs:137`) plus `use_environment::<T>()` that reads the `State`; a derive is not needed, because the type is the key. Write it into `crates/bk/examples/` | S | none |
| `isEnabled`, `.disabled(_:)` on a container | | implemented (was partial): `.disabled(bool)` and `use_is_enabled()` (`crates/bk/src/environment.rs:64`, [Disabled](../barrierkit/16-swiftui-to-the-kit.md#disabled)) | `.disabled(bool)` is a method of each control (19 control methods named `disabled` in `crates/bk/src`: Button, Switch, Slider, TextField, SearchField, SecureField, DatePicker, ColorPicker, ComboBox, PopUpButton, SegmentedControl, CircularSlider, choices...). No ambient value: `.disabled` on a `Form`, `Section` or `VStack` does not exist. `grep -rniE 'is_?enabled\|DisabledScope' crates/bk/src` → 0 hits as an ambient value (`enabled` flags inside controls only). | Disabling a whole form or panel while a task runs | A `Disabled` scope component that provides `State<bool>` through a context, and a kit-internal `use_enabled(self_disabled)` that every control calls; then `.disabled` as a `ViewModifiers` method | M | none |
| `Transaction`, `TransactionKey`, `transaction(_:)`, `withTransaction`, `withAnimation`, `Transaction.animation`, `.disablesAnimations`, `.addAnimationCompletion`, `.isContinuous`, `.tracksVelocity`, scroll members | | implemented (was absent): `with_animation`, `Animation`, implicit transactions (`crates/bk/src/animation.rs`, [Implicit animation](../barrierkit/14-animations.md#implicit-animation), recipe `with_animation.rs`); `TransactionKey` absent | `grep -rniE 'TransactionKey\|with_?animation\|disables_?animations\|Transaction\b' crates agents docs/barrierkit` → 0. Motion is per control: `styles::Animation` (None, Ease, Spring, Custom) in the style and `use_animated(target, animation)` (`animation.rs`, `pub(crate)`). The reduced-motion preference is applied last (ADR 0064). | `withAnimation { state = x }` on any state change; turn an animation off for one subtree; run code when an animation ends | A context flag read by `use_animated` (like `use_reduced_motion`), set by a `transaction` scope; completion callbacks on `Freya` animation end. A true `withAnimation` for arbitrary property changes has no host in Freya: the kit animates only the controls that call `use_animated` | M | design (macOS behavior of interrupted and nested transactions is not measured) |
| `Preview` (`#Preview`), `Previewable`, `PreviewModifier`, `PreviewModifierContent`, `PreviewProvider`, `previewLayout`, `previewDisplayName` | | partial (`Preview`), absent (others) | Nothing plays the role for an app author, and the kit has no offscreen renderer. `TestingRunner::render_to_file` (`freya-testing-0.5.3/src/lib.rs:622`) is Freya's primitive. `grep -rniE '#Preview\|PreviewProvider\|PreviewModifier\|preview_?layout\|hot_?reload\|subsecond\|dioxus' crates agents docs/barrierkit Cargo.toml dev.sh scripts` → 0. `freya-core` has a `hotreload` feature (`Cargo.toml` `[features]`; render wrapped in `subsecond::HotFn` at `src/element.rs:322-339`); the kit does not enable it (workspace `freya-core = "=0.5.3"`, default features). | See a view while writing it, in light and dark, at a size, with sample data | Static: a `bk::preview::render(view, size, scheme) -> png` that wraps `TestingRunner` plus `use_appearance`, exposed to apps and a `cargo run --example` convention. Live: Freya's `hotreload` feature needs a devtools connection that `freya-winit` starts, and ADR 0033 bans `freya-winit` | static M, live L | Freya (`hotreload` exists in `freya-core`, but its client is outside the pinned crates; `unverified` whether it works with the kit's own runner) |
| `preference(key:value:)`, `onPreferenceChange`, `anchorPreference`, `transformAnchorPreference`, `transformPreference`, `backgroundPreferenceValue`, `overlayPreferenceValue`, `PreferenceKey`, `PreferredColorSchemeKey` (merged below) | | absent | `grep -rniE 'PreferenceKey\|anchor_?preference\|on_?preference_?change\|preference_?value\|transform_?preference' crates agents docs/barrierkit` → 0. The data-up idiom the kit uses: a context holding a `State` that the child sets (`ToolbarItems`, `compact.rs:19-47`) or `on_sized` callbacks. | A child reports a size, a title or a bound to its ancestors (tab bar badges, a header that measures its content, popovers anchored to a child) | A `PreferenceKey` trait with `reduce`, a context holding the combined `State`, and `.preference(key, value)` that registers on mount and unregisters in `use_drop`. There is no per-window preferred scheme, only the subtree hook. | `isUserAuthenticationEnabled`, `scrollDismissesKeyboardMode`, `onHingeChange`, `PreviewContext`, `PreviewContextKey`, `previewContext`, `PreviewDevice`, `previewDevice`, `previewInterfaceOrientation`, `AnyLocation`, `AnyLocationBase` | 27.1 (hinge) | n/a | LocalAuthentication/Touch ID (Apple service); touch keyboard (no soft keyboard on a desktop; macOS compiles and ignores the call); foldable posture (0 hits for hinge, posture, foldable in the five `wayland-protocols*` crates); WidgetKit and iOS device catalog previews (deprecated); SwiftUI-internal location storage (Freya's `State`/`Writable` is the equivalent). | | | | |

### 7.2 Classification of every item (closed list)
types (56) - implemented (2): `State` (`use_state`), `LazyState` (`use_state` takes an init closure, run once); partial (23): `Bindable`, `Binding`, `BadgeProminence`, `ColorScheme`, `ColorSchemeContrast`, `ControlActiveState`, `DismissAction`, `DynamicProperty`, `Environment`, `EnvironmentKey`, `EnvironmentObject`, `EnvironmentValues`, `LayoutDirection`, `ListItemTint`, `MaterialActiveAppearance`, `ObservedObject`, `PreferredColorSchemeKey` (merged with `preferredColorScheme`), `PresentationMode`, `Preview`, `ScaledMetric`, `StateObject`, `SubscriptionView` (the view `onReceive` returns), `TimeDataSource`; absent (22): `AnimationContext`, `AnimationState`, `AnimationStateKey`, `BackgroundProminence`, `ContainerValueKey`, `ContainerValues`, `Entry`, `EnvironmentalModifier`, `LayoutDirectionBehavior`, `Namespace`, `PreferenceKey`, `Previewable`, `PreviewModifier`, `PreviewModifierContent`, `PreviewProvider`, `Prominence`, `RefreshAction`, `RenameAction`, `SystemFormatStyle`, `Transaction`, `TransactionKey`, `TypesettingLanguage`; n/a (5): `AnyLocation`, `AnyLocationBase` (SwiftUI-internal storage), `PreviewContext`, `PreviewContextKey` (WidgetKit timeline previews), `PreviewDevice` (iOS device catalog); out of scope (4): `FetchRequest`, `FetchedResults`, `SectionedFetchRequest`, `SectionedFetchResults` (matrix scope sentence, no ADR).
view modifiers (34) - implemented (2): `equatable` (`Component: PartialEq`), `id` (`KeyExt::key`); partial (14): `colorScheme` (deprecated), `defaultAppStorage`, `environment`, `environmentObject`, `modifier`, `onAppear`, `onChange` (deprecated), `onDisappear`, `onGeometryChange`, `onReceive`, `preferredColorScheme`, `tag`, `task`, `transformEnvironment`; absent (14): `anchorPreference`, `backgroundPreferenceValue`, `containerValue`, `handlesExternalEvents`, `layoutValue`, `onOpenURL`, `onPreferenceChange`, `overlayPreferenceValue`, `preference`, `previewDisplayName` (deprecated), `previewLayout` (deprecated), `transaction`, `transformAnchorPreference`, `transformPreference`; n/a (4): `onHingeChange` (since 27.1; foldable hardware), `previewContext`, `previewDevice`, `previewInterfaceOrientation` (deprecated, Xcode canvas and iOS only); out of scope (0): none.
static style members (0) - implemented: none; partial: none; absent: none; n/a: none; out of scope: none (the area has no `extension XStyle where Self ==` members).
environment values (14) - implemented (1): `controlSize` (`StyleModifiers::control_size`, matrix §11 `measured`; reading it from a custom view is not possible); partial (7): `calendar`, `colorScheme`, `colorSchemeContrast`, `displayScale`, `isEnabled`, `locale`, `pixelLength`; absent (4): `backgroundProminence`, `backgroundStyle`, `systemPrefersReducedResourceUsage` (since 27), `timeZone`; n/a (2): `isUserAuthenticationEnabled` (LocalAuthentication), `scrollDismissesKeyboardMode` (touch keyboard); out of scope (0): none.
Members added to the area's own types (Binding: `animation`, `endIndex`, `formIndex`, `id`, `index`, `indices`, `startIndex`, `transaction`; `Bindable.id`; `DynamicProperty.update`; `FetchRequest.update`, `FetchedResults.sortDescriptors`, the two sectioned ones; `PreferenceKey.defaultValue`; `PreviewModifier.makeSharedContext`; `PreviewProvider.platform`; `TimeDataSource` `currentDate`, `dateRange`, `durationOffset`; the 9 `Transaction` members) follow their type: partial for Binding, DynamicProperty and TimeDataSource members, absent for PreferenceKey, PreviewModifier, PreviewProvider and Transaction, out of scope for the Core Data ones. Not counted in the 104.

### 7.3 Area priorities
1. `Binding` (and `$model.field`): every form, settings pane and model-driven view is written with it; the kit's controlled callbacks work but cost a closure per control and no sub-field projection. Size M. Blocker none (Freya `Writable<T>` is the base).
2. `Environment`/`environment(_:_:)` for app-defined values: apps cannot pass a document, a user or a model down without `use_provide_context` plus a wrapper component, and the value is not reactive unless it is a `State`. A `Provide<T>` and `use_environment` plus a recipe. Size S. Blocker none.
3. Ambient `isEnabled` / `.disabled(_:)` on containers: a form that is disabled while a task runs needs 20 separate `.disabled` calls today. Size M. Blocker none.
4. `Transaction` / `withAnimation`: state-driven animation is the SwiftUI default; the kit animates only inside controls. Size M. Blocker design (macOS semantics of nested and interrupted transactions are unmeasured).
5. `Preview` (`#Preview`): there is no way to see a view while writing it. A static `render(view, size, scheme)` over `freya-testing` is M; hot reload is L. Blocker Freya (`hotreload` feature exists, its client is outside the pinned crates).
6. `timeZone` / `calendar` / `locale` (and the UTC `CivilDate::today()` bug): dates and number formatting are wrong or missing for every app outside UTC. Size M. Blocker none.
7. `PreferenceKey` family: child-to-ancestor data (measured headers, tab badges, anchored popovers) has no generic route. Size M. Blocker none (sibling order unverified).
8. Lifecycle names (`onAppear`, `onDisappear`, `onChange`, `task`) as recipes or `.on_appear`/`.on_disappear` methods: the hooks exist, but no recipe or glossary row tells an app author which Freya hook to use. Size S. Blocker none.

### 7.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified
- Rows the matrix lacks. It has no state or data-flow section: its scope excludes "data ... frameworks", and `swiftui-appkit-coverage.md` lists only `systemPrefersReducedResourceUsage` (§18, P3, `missing`; this study agrees). Proposed rows (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `@State` | — | `use_state` (Freya, glossary "Freya names you will meet") | implemented | —
  - `@Binding`, `Bindable` | `NSObject.bind(_:to:)` ≈ | none; Freya `Writable<T>` | partial | P2
  - `@Environment`, `.environment(_:_:)` | `NSAppearance` ≈ | `use_consume`, `use_provide_context`, `theme::resolved()` | partial | P2
  - `.disabled(_:)` as environment (`isEnabled`) | `NSControl.isEnabled` | per-control `.disabled(bool)` | partial | P2
  - `Transaction`, `withAnimation` | `NSAnimationContext.runAnimationGroup` | `Animation` in styles | missing | P2
  - `#Preview` | — | goldens and `doc_shots` (test only) | partial | P2
  - `timeZone`, `calendar`, `locale` | `NSCalendar`, `NSLocale`, `TimeZone` | none; `CivilDate::today()` is UTC | partial | P2
  - `PreferenceKey` family | — | none | missing | P3
  - `onAppear`, `onDisappear`, `onChange`, `task`, `onReceive` | — | `use_hook`, `use_drop`, `use_side_effect_with_deps`, `spawn`/`use_future`, `use_published` | partial | P3
  - `onOpenURL`, `handlesExternalEvents` | `application(_:open:)` | D-Bus `Open` drops its URIs | missing | P3
  - `DismissAction` | `NSWindow.close`, `endSheet` | `WindowControls::close`, `PopupHandle::close` | partial | P3
  - `ColorScheme`, `preferredColorScheme` | `NSApp.appearance` | `theme::use_appearance`, `ctappearance::ColorScheme` | partial | P3
  - `ScaledMetric`, `displayScale`, `pixelLength` | `backingScaleFactor` | `Font::scaled`, Freya `scale_factor` | partial | P3
- Rows this study disagrees with, or doc points that differ: (a) glossary line 1289 gives `app::use_defaults` the SwiftUI counterpart `AppStorage` without `≈`; it returns a read-only `Memo<T>` of a whole TOML struct and writes go through `update_defaults`, so `@AppStorage` (a per-key read-write binding) is only approximated. (b) The brief's hint "no automatic dependency tracking" does not hold for Freya: `State::read` subscribes the running component, memo, effect or future (`freya-core/src/lifecycle/state.rs:306`, `memo.rs:29`, `effect.rs`); what is missing is per-property granularity and property-wrapper syntax. (c) `docs/barrierkit/04-appearance-and-tokens.md:322` imports `ctappearance::ColorScheme` in an app snippet, against ADR 0061. (d) `docs/barrierkit/13-app.md` says `Open` URIs are dropped, so `onOpenURL` is absent as claimed. (e) The date picker's Today button uses the UTC date (`date.rs:141`) while the appearance schedule uses local time (`ctappearance/src/config.rs:381`).
- Misfiled input items: `AnimationContext`, `AnimationState`, `AnimationStateKey` (animation), `BadgeProminence` and `ListItemTint` (lists and badges), `LayoutDirection` and `LayoutDirectionBehavior` (layout), `MaterialActiveAppearance` (materials), `RenameAction` (navigation), `SystemFormatStyle`, `TimeDataSource` and `TypesettingLanguage` (text), `layoutValue` (layout; its `LayoutValueKey` sits in the containers file), `onGeometryChange` (layout), `handlesExternalEvents` and `onOpenURL` (scenes), `isUserAuthenticationEnabled` (security), `scrollDismissesKeyboardMode` (scroll views), `controlSize`, `backgroundStyle` (controls and shape styles), `defaultAppStorage` (its `AppStorage` is in D_scenes). `AppStorage` and `SceneStorage` are in D_scenes, `FocusState`, `FocusedValue`, `FocusedBinding`, `FocusedObject` and `GestureState` in E_input, `AccessibilityFocusState` in H_access, `Anchor` in G_gfx: not classified here. Kit mapping for the lead: `AppStorage` ≈ `use_defaults`/`update_defaults` (read-only `Memo`), `SceneStorage` ≈ `load_restoration`/`save_restoration` (whole struct, no per-scene key), `FocusState` ≈ Freya `use_focus`/`use_a11y` and `Interaction::focused`.
- Idiom map (hooks the kit and Freya give). `@State` = `use_state`; `@State` with computed value = `use_memo` (`Memo<T>`); `onChange` = `use_side_effect_with_deps`; derived props to state = `use_reactive(&self.prop)`; app-wide state = `State::create_global` (31 lines, mostly tests and internals) or a root context (`use_provide_root_context`); `ObservableObject` = a struct of `State` fields. The kit adds what SwiftUI lacks: `use_published`/`Publisher`/`use_wake` hand values from any thread (`app/published.rs`); `use_watched`/`use_defaults` reload when a settings file changes in another process; `use_undo` wires `UndoManager` to the Undo and Redo menu items (`app/undo.rs`); `use_controlled` keeps a control's own value while the pointer holds it (`controlled.rs`, `pub(crate)`); `use_timeline` (`TimelineView`). Lost: property wrappers and `$` syntax, `Binding`, per-property observation, an environment bag with key paths, `PreferenceKey`, `Transaction`, `@Observable`.
- Not verified: whether Freya's `hotreload` feature can work with the kit's own runner; whether Freya exposes tree order for preference reduction; whether a child's props can be read from an `Element` (container values); whether Freya's paragraph accepts a language (`TypesettingLanguage`); `use_future` cancellation on unmount (`spawn` is documented to cancel on unmount, `task.rs:87`; `use_future` spawns through `spawn` in the same way, not run); macOS behavior of `backgroundProminence` inks (no probe).

## 8. Animation, graphics, shapes, color and materials

Counts of the closed list: types 142 (11 implemented, 37 partial, 91 absent, 3 n/a); view modifiers 67 (6, 18, 42, 0, 1 out of scope); static style members 65 (7, 21, 37, 0); environment values 11 (0, 2, 7, 1, 1 out of scope). The gaps dominate: 91 of 142 types and 42 of 67 modifiers are absent. The kit covers the look of controls, not SwiftUI as a drawing language: there is no `Shape` view, no `Transition`, no keyframes, no content filters and no `glassEffect` on an arbitrary view. What it has is a closed `ShapeStyle` enum, `Gradient`, `Thickness` materials, `GlassContainer`, a Skia `Canvas`, `Spring` and `Animation`. Freya 0.5.3 gives for free: per-element `opacity`, `rotate`, `scale` with `transform_origin`, `corner_radius` (with smoothing), box `shadow`, `border`, linear/radial/conic gradient fills, `Fill::Shader` (SkSL, fills only), `Fill::Callback`, `backdrop_blur`, `overflow(Clip)`, `image()` with `AspectRatio` and `SamplingMode`, and time-based `AnimNum`/`AnimColor`/`AnimSequential` (`freya-animation-0.5.3`). The main hard blocker is Freya's flat renderer: `freya-core-0.5.3/src/render_pipeline.rs:59-207` draws each node alone, with no begin/end hook around a subtree, so anything that needs the subtree in an offscreen layer (content blur, color filters, blend modes, masks, `compositingGroup`, `layerEffect`) needs a Freya patch. Skia itself (`freya-skia-safe-0.104.0`) has all the pieces (`ColorMatrix`, `image_filters::runtime_shader`, `PathMeasure`, `TrimPathEffect`, `pathops`, `draw_patch`, `Vertices`); the kit already uses `RuntimeEffect` in `glass_container.rs` and `app_icon.rs`.

### 8.3 Area priorities
1. `Animation` presets and a physical `Spring` (with the implicit `animation(_:value:)` hook): every state change in a SwiftUI app uses them, and today the kit has one fixed-duration curve and a crate-private `use_animated`; M plus S; measurement.
2. `Shape` views with `fill`, `stroke`, `trim`, `StrokeStyle` and `Path`: icons, rings, charts and any custom outline need a drawable shape that lays out like a view; M; none.
3. `.transition` with `matchedGeometryEffect`: appearing and disappearing views and shared-element moves are what make apps feel native; M; none.
4. `glassEffect` on an arbitrary view with free layout and `Glass.tint/.clear/.interactive`: the macOS 26 look; `GlassContainer` is a row only; M; measurement.
5. `ConcentricRectangle` and `containerShape`: nested rounded corners are the macOS 26 geometry; M; the rule is measured ([macOS corners](macos-corners.md#7-the-concentric-rule)).
6. Subtree layers (`blur`, color filters, `blendMode`, `compositingGroup`, `mask`, `layerEffect`): one Freya render-pipeline patch unlocks about 15 modifiers; L; Freya.
7. `Material` values and the adaptive `Color` palette: apps use `.regularMaterial` and `.red` everywhere and the kit's values are unmeasured; M; measurement.
8. `ContentTransition.numericText` and `symbolEffect`: counters and icon feedback; M; design.

### 8.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified
- Matrix §17 Motion row `Animation`: says the window, Dock and menu timings were observed; `macos-animations.md` §1 shows those are desktop animations (menu instant, window 250 ms default, nothing for `.smooth`/`.snappy`/`.bouncy`). The row should say that no SwiftUI `Animation` preset was measured. Same sheet, row `.transition`, `matchedGeometryEffect`, `PhaseAnimator`, `KeyframeAnimator`: agree (`missing`); this study adds `contentTransition`, `scrollTransition`, `visualEffect`, `TimelineView`.
- Matrix §15 row `GlassEffectContainer`, `glassEffectID` marked `implemented`: this study rates it `partial`. `GlassContainer` is a row layout with static `&'static str` ids and at most 8 shapes; no `glassEffectUnion`, no `glassEffectTransition`, no free layout. Matrix §1 row `.glass` says "the compositor draws the glass", but `ButtonStyle::glass` (`styles/button.rs:151`) is a client fill (`Role::RaisedBackground`) with a hairline rim and a capsule; the compositor glass is only for window panels.
- Matrix §16 `.symbolRenderingMode` row `partial`: this study agrees for monochrome only; `.symbolVariant`, `.symbolEffect`, variable value: agree `missing`.
- Rows the matrix lacks (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri): `Shape` views, `Path`, `StrokeStyle` | `NSBezierPath` | `styles::Shape` outline only | partial | P2; `blur`, color filters, `blendMode`, `compositingGroup`, `mask` | `CIFilter`, `compositingFilter` | none | absent | P2; `ConcentricRectangle`, `containerShape` | AppKit equivalent not checked | none | absent | P2; `Color` named system colors | `NSColor.systemRed` and siblings | none | absent | P1; gradients (`RadialGradient`, `AngularGradient`, `MeshGradient`) | `NSGradient` | `styles::Gradient` linear | partial | P3; `Canvas` | `NSView.draw` | `canvas::Canvas` | partial | P3; `ImageRenderer` | `NSBitmapImageRep` | none | absent | P3; `Shader`, `layerEffect` | `CIKernel` | none | absent | P3 (candidate out of scope); `transformEffect`, `rotation3DEffect` | `CATransform3D` | Freya `rotate`, `scale` | partial | P3; HDR `allowedDynamicRange` | `NSImage.preferredDynamicRange` | none | absent | P3 (candidate out of scope).
- Misfiled items: `ScrollPhaseChangeContext` (scroll area, goes with `onScrollPhaseChange`); `DefaultDateProgressLabel` (`ProgressView(timerInterval:)`, gauges area); `GlassButtonStyle` and `GlassProminentButtonStyle` (buttons area, matrix §1); `containerBackground`, `scrollContentBackground` (windows and scroll areas); `overlayArrangementEdge` (27.1, with `ArrangementView`, matrix §18); `hidden`, `overlay`, `background`, `offset`, `opacity`, `border`, `cornerRadius`, `clipped`, `shadow` (view basics, in this area by regex); `TimelineView`, `AsyncImage` (live in `SwiftUI`, not `SwiftUICore`, but are graphics or time); `Chirality` (hand handedness for visionOS spatial input; no declaration in either interface uses it, unverified).
- Naming: Freya already uses `opacity`, `rotate`, `scale`, `shadow`, `border`, `background`, `corner_radius`, `overflow`; the kit forwards them through `bk::prelude` (`freya_core::prelude::*`). The glossary lists none of them.
- Unverified: SwiftUI spring formulas and preset constants (`.smooth`, `.snappy`, `.bouncy`) are taken from Apple's public documentation, not from a probe; whether macOS ever sets `isLuminanceReduced` (always-on display concept, this study classifies `n/a`); whether the non-smoothed `clip_rrect` of Freya's `RectElement::clip` is visible at continuous corners; whether Freya's `rotate` and `scale` also move hit testing (not read); whether `Rect::backdrop_blur` with `corner_radius` samples the real window (the kit's `glass.rs` comment at line 112 says an opacity layer breaks it).

## 9. Accessibility

Closed list: 28 types, 43 view modifiers, 0 static style members, 19 environment values. Types: 0 implemented, 7 partial, 5 absent, 16 n/a. Modifiers: 0 implemented, 14 partial, 11 absent, 18 n/a. Environment values: 1 implemented, 6 partial, 8 absent, 4 n/a. The gaps are not drawing gaps: the kit already publishes a real AT-SPI tree, but an app author can only name, describe or hide a view through Freya's raw `a11y_*` methods, and only on 10 of the kit's views (`Text`, `VStack`, `HStack`, `Badge`, `Divider`, `Avatar`, `Scrim`, `SymbolTile`, `ContentUnavailableView`, `NavigationButtons`). Freya 0.5.3 gives the whole AccessKit tree for free (every element becomes a node, `a11y_role`, `a11y_alt`, `a11y_builder` with the raw `accesskit::Node`, focus and groups), but it also blocks three things: it overwrites a node's label with its own or its first child's text, it emits no text runs (no AT-SPI Text interface), and it builds the tree 1:1 from the view tree. A second blocker sits one layer lower: `accesskit_atspi_common` 0.21.1 exposes one AT-SPI action ("click"), no `LabelledBy` relation, no expanded or popup state and no heading level. "partial" below means: reachable only through those raw Freya methods on those 10 views, or through the `label` argument of a control's `new`.

### 9.1 Gaps and discrepancies
| SwiftUI API (family) | Since | Status | Kit today | Used for | Rust/Freya way | Size | Blocker |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `accessibilityLabel`, `accessibilityValue`, `accessibilityHint`, `accessibilityHidden`, deprecated `accessibility(label/hint/value/identifier:)` (5 + 1) | | partial | The `label` argument of every control's `new` is the AT-SPI name (docs 05-controls "Common rules"). `Button`, `Switch`, `Checkbox`, `Slider`, `TextField`, `Picker` and the other controls have no `a11y_*` method: `forward_element!` is used by 10 views only (`crates/bk/src/elements.rs:129-163`, `adornments.rs`, `media.rs`, `sidebar.rs:193`, `views/feedback.rs:67`, `views/navigation.rs:72`). `grep -rn 'AccessibilityExt' crates/bk/src` shows only that macro and `lib.rs`. Glossary has no `accessibility_*` row. | Give a control a name different from its visible text, add a hint, hide decoration | A trait implemented by every control with `accessibility_label`, `accessibility_hint`, `accessibility_value`, `accessibility_hidden(bool)`; each stores an override applied after the control's own `a11y_alt` on its root node. Hint maps to AccessKit `description` (AT-SPI Accessible.Description, `accesskit_atspi_common` `node.rs:46`). Hidden maps to `Node::set_hidden` (excluded with its subtree, `accesskit_consumer` `filters.rs:22`). Value is numeric only in AT-SPI (`set_numeric_value`, Value interface); a string value has no AT-SPI field. | M | Freya: `freya-core-0.5.3/src/accessibility/tree.rs` `create_node` ("Set inner text") replaces the label with the node's own or first child's text for every role except `Label`, so an override that differs from the visible text is lost on text-bearing nodes (code reading; not run) |
| Text input accessibility (`TextField`, `SecureField`, `TextEditor`; no modifier, but the base of `accessibilityTextContentType` and every form) | | partial | `TextField`/`SearchField` take a `placeholder`, not a label. The node name is the placeholder while empty and the typed text otherwise (`freya-components-0.5.3/src/input.rs:720-724`); there is no separate name plus value. No Text or EditableText interface: AccessKit needs `TextRun` children (`accesskit_consumer` `text.rs:1483`), and `grep -rn TextRun freya-core-0.5.3/src` finds none. Orca cannot read caret, selection or line. `SecureField` and `TextEditor` set role and label by hand (`controls/text_field.rs:98`, `text_editor.rs:434-440`). | Orca announces field name, caret movement, selected text | Emit `Role::TextRun` children with character lengths and `set_text_selection`; accept `SetValue`, `SetTextSelection` actions | L | Freya: no text runs in `create_node`; `Input` owns the a11y of the field (`input.rs:720-735`) |
| `accessibilityAction`, `accessibilityActions`, `accessibilityAdjustableAction`, `accessibilityScrollAction`, `accessibilityRespondsToUserInteraction`, `AccessibilityActionKind`, `AccessibilityAdjustmentDirection` | | partial (`.default` only) | `on_press` adds the `Click` action (`freya-core-0.5.3/src/elements/extensions.rs:362-368`). The runner honors only `Focus` and `Click` and drops the rest (`platform/app.rs` `take_bridge_actions`, `_ => continue`; docs 18-platform "Other actions are ignored"). `AccessibilityTree::handle_action` and `on_accessibility_action` are not used: `grep -rn 'handle_action\|on_accessibility_action' crates` → 0. So Orca cannot set a slider or stepper value, scroll an item into view or run a named action. | Custom VoiceOver-style actions ("Delete", "Reply"), adjust a slider by action, scroll a list item into view | Call `handle_action` for non-Click requests so `on_accessibility_action` handlers fire; add `accessibility_action(kind, handler)` and wire `SetValue` into `Slider`, `Stepper`, `DatePicker`, `ScrollIntoView` into scroll views. Named custom actions cannot reach Orca: AT-SPI gets one action named "click" (`node.rs:525-535`), `Increment`/`Decrement` have no AT-SPI call (Value.SetCurrentValue becomes `SetValue`). | S (routing `SetValue`, `ScrollIntoView`) | Freya for named actions: `accesskit_atspi_common-0.21.1/src/node.rs:525-535` exposes `n_actions` 0 or 1 |
| `accessibilityElement(children:)` (`.ignore`, `.contain`, `.combine`), `AccessibilityChildBehavior` | | partial | The AT tree mirrors the view tree (`tree.rs` `create_node` takes `tree.children`). A bare `rect` is `GenericContainer` and AccessKit filters it out, so unnamed containers are transparent (`accesskit_consumer` `filters.rs:29-31`). `.contain` is `a11y_role(Group)` plus a label (kit `Group`, `buttons.rs:266`). No `.combine`: a row announces its first text child only. | Make a card or a table row one announced element | `accessibility_element(children:)`: `.contain` sets role Group; `.ignore` sets hidden on descendants; `.combine` sets the label to the descendants' texts and hides them | M | Freya: same label overwrite; combined text must come from the app |
| `accessibilityChildren`, `accessibilityRepresentation` | | absent | `grep -rniE 'accessibility_?(children|representation)' crates docs/barrierkit` → 0. The AT subtree is derived from the drawn elements, so it cannot be replaced. | Custom-drawn charts, canvases and gauges described as real controls | Draw the view, hide it, and place an invisible `rect` sibling carrying the AT subtree | L | Freya: `tree.rs` `create_node` builds children from `tree.children` only |
| `accessibilityLabeledPair`, `accessibilityLinkedGroup`, `AccessibilityLabeledPairRole`; form row labels (`LabeledContent` = kit `Row`) | | absent | `grep -rniE 'labelled_by\|labeled_by\|described_by' crates` → 0. A `Row` title is not tied to the control in it, so Orca reads "text entry" without the row title. AccessKit has `Node::set_labelled_by` and uses it for the name (`accesskit_consumer` `node.rs:731-780`), but AT-SPI exports only `ControllerFor` as a relation (`accesskit_atspi_common` `node.rs:972-990`). | Tie a visible label to its field or switch (WCAG 1.3.1, 4.1.2) | `Row` gives the control's `AccessibilityId` and calls `set_labelled_by`; or set the control's label to the row title | S | none for buttons, switches, sliders; Freya for text fields (the field's own label always wins) |
| `accessibilityAddTraits`, `accessibilityRemoveTraits`, `AccessibilityTraits` | | partial | `a11y_role` plus raw flags through `a11y_builder` (`set_toggled`, `set_selected`, `set_expanded`, `set_has_popup`, `set_numeric_value`) in the kit's own controls (e.g. `controls/toggle.rs:224-226`). Apps get them only on the 10 forwarding views. Traits with no AccessKit or AT-SPI field (`updatesFrequently`, `playsSound`, `startsMediaSession`, `allowsDirectInteraction`, `causesPageTurn`, `isKeyboardKey`, `isSummaryElement`) have no target. | Say "this is a button / header / selected / modal" | `AccessibilityTraits` option set mapped to `Role`, `Toggled`, selected, modal; unmappable traits are no-ops | S | none |
| `accessibilityHeading`, `AccessibilityHeadingLevel` | | partial | `grep -rniE 'heading' crates --include=*.rs` → 0: no kit view sets `Role::Heading`, so Orca's heading navigation finds none (form `Section` titles, `Text` headlines). AccessKit has `Role::Heading` (mapped, `node.rs`) and `set_level`; the AT-SPI adapter exports no level (`attributes()` lists placeholder-text, posinset, setsize, id, braillelabel, brailleroledescription). | Heading structure for screen reader navigation (WCAG 1.3.1, 2.4.6) | `Text::accessibility_heading(level)` sets role Heading and level; `Section` header uses it | S | none (level not exported: upstream adapter) |
| `accessibilityIdentifier` | | partial | `ProbeExt::probe(id)` on `Rect` only (`crates/bk/src/lib.rs`, `impl ProbeExt for Rect`; glossary row `ProbeExt`) sets `author_id`; the AT-SPI adapter does not export `author_id` (only `html_id` as attribute "id", `node.rs`). Tests find nodes by it (`tests/sidebar.rs:173`). | UI test selectors | Also set `html_id`; put the method on the same trait as row 1 | S | none |
| `accessibilityFocused`, `accessibilityDefaultFocus` (26), `AccessibilityFocusState` | 26 | partial | `use_focus(AccessibilityId)`, `request_focus()`, `a11y_auto_focus` (`TextField::auto_focus`); the AT cursor is the keyboard focus (`AccessibilityTree::focused_id`), one cursor, unlike VoiceOver's. An AT `Focus` action moves it (`app.rs` `take_bridge_actions`). No `AccessibilityFocusState`-named wrapper or binding. | Move the screen reader to an error message or a new sheet | Hook returning a `State<Option<T>>` bound to Freya focus ids (`FocusState` is another area) | S | none |
| Expanded and popup state (`DisclosureGroup`, `Menu`, `PopUpButton`; no modifier) | | partial | The kit sets `set_expanded` (`views/group.rs:139`, `views/tree.rs:285`, `sidebar.rs:655`) and `set_has_popup(Menu)` (`buttons.rs:88,234`). `grep -rniE 'expand\|has_popup' accesskit_atspi_common-0.21.1/src accesskit_unix-0.24.1/src` → 0: neither reaches AT-SPI, so Orca never says expanded, collapsed or "menu". | Disclosure and menu state | Upstream patch to AccessKit AT-SPI (States Expandable, Expanded; Role description) or `set_role_description` as a text fallback | M | Freya (dependency `accesskit_atspi_common` 0.21.1) |
| `accessibilitySortPriority` | | absent | `grep -rniE 'sort_?priority' crates` → 0. AT-SPI order is the child order; Freya's is the element order. | Reading order that differs from draw order | Reorder the element tree, or a Freya patch to sort children | L | Freya: `create_node` children order |
| `redacted(reason:)`, `unredacted`, `RedactionReasons`, `redactionReasons` | | absent | `grep -rniE 'redact\|skeleton\|shimmer' crates docs/barrierkit` → 0. | Loading skeletons: text and shapes drawn as gray bars until data arrives | Context value set by `.redacted(reason:)`; `Text`, `Image`, shapes read it and draw a rounded bar in a placeholder fill; hide the subtree from AT | M | measurement (macOS placeholder fill and radius not captured) |

Freya's `ScreenReader` context exists and nothing sets it (`platform/app.rs:158`). | Skip decorative animation, show longer help when a screen reader runs | `use_accessibility_enabled()` hook: read `org.a11y.Status` `IsEnabled`, `ScreenReaderEnabled` on the a11y bus (`org.a11y.Bus.GetAddress` on the session bus, zbus is already a dependency) | S | none |

Show Borders reaches icon buttons, tool groups and search fields (`styles/theme.rs:24`, docs 04:422-427). Differentiate Without Color reaches `TrafficLights` only. The compositor applies Invert Colors; no control reads it.
