### 9.2 Classification of every item (closed list)
types (28) - implemented (0): none; partial (7): `AccessibilityTraits`, `AccessibilityChildBehavior`, `AccessibilityFocusState`, `AccessibilityHeadingLevel`, `AccessibilityActionKind`, `DynamicTypeSize`, `ContentSizeCategory` (merged with its sibling, the size type listed before it: both map to `Resolved::text_size`); absent (5): `AccessibilityAdjustmentDirection`, `AccessibilityLabeledPairRole`, `LegibilityWeight`, `RedactionReasons`, `DisplayProxy` (misfiled, windows area); n/a (16): `AXChartDescriptorRepresentable` (Audio Graphs are VoiceOver only; the kit has no Chart; Orca has no equivalent), `AccessibilityActionCategory`, `AccessibilityCustomContentKey`, `AccessibilityRotorContent`, `AccessibilityRotorContentBuilder`, `AccessibilityRotorEntry`, `AccessibilitySystemRotor` (VoiceOver rotors: Orca navigates by role, which `a11y_role` already sets; AT-SPI has no custom rotor), `AccessibilityDirectTouchOptions`, `AccessibilityZoomGestureAction` (touch and trackpad VoiceOver gestures; AT-SPI has none), `AccessibilityTextContentType` (no AT-SPI field), `AccessibilityAttachmentModifier` (return-type plumbing of SwiftUI's modifiers; Rust has no `ModifiedContent`), `AccessibilityTechnologies` (no public signature in the two interfaces uses it), `AccessibilityQuickActionStyle`, `AccessibilityQuickActionPromptStyle`, `AccessibilityQuickActionOutlineStyle` (macOS-unavailable: watchOS), `UserInterfaceSizeClass` (iPhone and iPad compact adaptation; the kit has no compact layout; macOS value unverified); out of scope (0): none
view modifiers (43) - implemented (0): none; partial (14): `accessibilityLabel`, `accessibilityValue`, `accessibilityHint`, `accessibilityHidden`, `accessibility` (deprecated; merged with the four siblings), `accessibilityAddTraits`, `accessibilityRemoveTraits`, `accessibilityElement`, `accessibilityHeading`, `accessibilityIdentifier`, `accessibilityAction` (`.default` only), `accessibilityRespondsToUserInteraction`, `accessibilityFocused`, `accessibilityDefaultFocus`; absent (11): `accessibilityActions`, `accessibilityAdjustableAction`, `accessibilityScrollAction`, `accessibilityChildren`, `accessibilityRepresentation`, `accessibilityLabeledPair`, `accessibilityLinkedGroup`, `accessibilitySortPriority`, `accessibilityIgnoresInvertColors`, `redacted`, `unredacted`; n/a (18): `accessibilityActivationPoint` (AT-SPI has no activation point; Orca routes the pointer to the node's extents, unverified), `accessibilityChartDescriptor` (Audio Graphs), `accessibilityCustomContent` (VoiceOver "more content"; fold into `description`), `accessibilityDirectTouch`, `accessibilityZoomAction`, `accessibilityDragPoint`, `accessibilityDropPoint` (touch, trackpad and drag gestures of VoiceOver; AT-SPI has no drag-and-drop interface), `accessibilityInputLabels` (Voice Control aliases; Linux has no system voice control and AT-SPI carries only the name), `accessibilityRotor`, `accessibilityRotorEntry` (rotors), `accessibilityScrollStatus` (26; spoken scroll position text, no AT-SPI field), `accessibilityShowsLargeContentViewer` (iOS and iPadOS bar feature), `accessibilityTextContentType`, `privacySensitive` (widgets and Lock Screen; no Linux counterpart), `speechAdjustedPitch`, `speechAlwaysIncludesPunctuation`, `speechSpellsOutCharacters`, `speechAnnouncementsQueued` (Orca and speech-dispatcher own speech; AT-SPI has no per-node speech attributes); out of scope (0): none
static style members (0) - none in the input file
environment values (19) - implemented (1): `accessibilityReduceMotion` (`animation::use_reduced_motion`, `crates/bk/src/animation.rs:20`; matrix §17); partial (6): `accessibilityReduceTransparency`, `accessibilityDifferentiateWithoutColor`, `accessibilityInvertColors`, `accessibilityShowBorders`, `accessibilityShowButtonShapes` (deprecated alias of the Show Borders value listed before it, merged), `sizeCategory` (deprecated; merged with `DynamicTypeSize` as `Resolved::text_size`); absent (8): `accessibilityEnabled`, `accessibilityVoiceOverEnabled`, `accessibilityDimFlashingLights`, `accessibilityPlayAnimatedImages`, `accessibilityPrefersCrossFadeTransitions` (26.4), `accessibilityReduceHighlightingEffects` (26.4), `legibilityWeight`, `redactionReasons`; n/a (4): `accessibilityAssistiveAccessEnabled` (Apple's simplified-UI mode of iOS and iPadOS), `accessibilityLargeContentViewerEnabled` (iOS and iPadOS), `accessibilityQuickActionsEnabled` (Apple Watch hardware), `accessibilitySwitchControlEnabled` (no Linux Switch Control; AT-SPI cannot tell which assistive technology is connected); out of scope (0): none
members added to the area's own types: `AXChartDescriptorRepresentable.updateChartDescriptor`, `AccessibilityChildBehavior.combine/contain/ignore`, `AccessibilityLabeledPairRole.hash/hashValue`, `AccessibilityRotorEntry.body` follow the status of their type.
Recount: types 0+7+5+16 = 28; modifiers 0+14+11+18 = 43; environment 1+6+8+4 = 19.

### 9.3 Area priorities
1. A public `accessibility_*` modifier set on every kit control (`accessibilityLabel`, `Hint`, `Value`, `Hidden`, `Identifier`): today only 10 views accept `a11y_*`, so an app cannot name or hide a `Button`, `Slider` or `Picker` differently from its visible text. Size M. Blocker Freya (label overwrite in `create_node`).
2. Text input accessibility (separate name and value, Text and EditableText interfaces): every form has fields, and Orca cannot read caret or selection. Size L. Blocker Freya (no text runs; `Input` owns its a11y).
3. AT-SPI action routing (`SetValue`, `ScrollIntoView`, `.default`, adjustable): sliders and steppers cannot be driven by a screen reader. Size S. Blocker none for routing; Freya for named custom actions.
4. Row and field labels (`accessibilityLabeledPair`, `LabeledContent`): settings forms are announced as unnamed controls. Size S. Blocker none except text fields.
5. `accessibilityHeading` and the expanded/popup states: structure and state that Orca users navigate by. Size S; expanded and popup need an upstream AT-SPI adapter patch. Blocker Freya (dependency).
6. Settings sources on Linux (`org.freedesktop.appearance`, `org.a11y.Status`): `accessibilityEnabled`, `VoiceOverEnabled`, Increase Contrast and Reduce Motion outside malaysia. Size M. Blocker none.
7. `accessibilityElement(children:)` with `.combine` and `.contain`: card and row grouping. Size M. Blocker Freya.
8. `redacted(reason: .placeholder)`: loading skeletons are common in SwiftUI apps. Size M. Blocker measurement.

### 9.4 Matrix drift, misfiled items, unverified
- **Disagree with matrix §17 row "VoiceOver" (`implemented`)**: propose `partial`. The tree, roles and focus work, but only `Focus` and `Click` are honored, no Text interface exists, expanded and popup state are not exported, and an author has no public modifiers on controls. The row cites `platform/atspi.rs` (now `platform/linux/atspi.rs`) only; the feed is `platform/app.rs` `update` and `take_bridge_actions`.
- **Rows the matrix lacks** (SwiftUI | AppKit | Kit | Status | Pri):
  - `accessibilityLabel/Value/Hint/Hidden/Identifier` | `NSAccessibilityProtocol` label, value, help, `isAccessibilityElement` | raw `a11y_*` on 10 views | partial | P1
  - `accessibilityAction`, `accessibilityAdjustableAction` | `accessibilityPerformPress/Increment`, custom actions | `Click` and `Focus` only | partial | P1
  - Text input accessibility | `NSAccessibilityTextArea` role, `accessibilityInsertionPointLineNumber` | name is placeholder or text, no Text interface | partial | P1
  - `accessibilityLabeledPair`, `accessibilityLinkedGroup` | `accessibilityTitleUIElement` | none | absent | P2
  - `accessibilityElement(children:)`, `accessibilityChildren`, `accessibilityRepresentation` | `accessibilityChildren` | tree mirrors view tree | partial/absent | P2
  - `accessibilityHeading` | `NSAccessibility.Role.heading`, `accessibilityLevel` | none | partial | P2
  - `redacted`, `unredacted`, `RedactionReasons` | none | none | absent | P2
  - `accessibilityEnabled`, `accessibilityVoiceOverEnabled` | `NSWorkspace.isVoiceOverEnabled` | none | absent | P3
  - `accessibilityDimFlashingLights`, `PlayAnimatedImages`, `PrefersCrossFadeTransitions`, `ReduceHighlightingEffects`, `legibilityWeight` | `NSWorkspace.accessibilityDisplayShould...` | none | absent | P3
  - `AccessibilityFocusState` | `NSAccessibility.post(.focusedUIElementChanged)` | `use_focus` | partial | P3
  - `DisplayProxy` | `NSScreen.frame`, `visibleFrame` | none | absent | P3
- **Misfiled**: `DisplayProxy` belongs to windows (`WindowPlacementContext`). The modifier `dynamicTypeSize(_:)` and the environment value `dynamicTypeSize` are in `A_text.txt` (lines 63 and 135); this study classified only the type `DynamicTypeSize` and its deprecated siblings. `ScaledMetric` is state and is not classified.
- **Doc claims that the code does not back**: `docs/research/styling-apple.md:398` says the user settings "come from `org.freedesktop.appearance` and the portal"; the code reads only malaysia's `appearance.domain` (`grep org.freedesktop.appearance crates` → 0). `docs/barrierkit/05-controls.md` says "the label of `new` is the accessible name"; for `TextField` and `SearchField` the argument is a placeholder, and the name becomes the typed text.
- **AT-SPI exposure today** (kit with `a11y` feature; read from `crates/bk/src/platform/{app,atspi}.rs`, `accesskit_unix` 0.24.1, `accesskit_atspi_common` 0.21.1). The adapter starts per toplevel window and is active only when the a11y bus says `IsEnabled`; layer, lock and popup surfaces publish nothing (docs 18-platform).
  - Interfaces per node: Accessible always; Component for every node with bounds (Freya sets bounds on all); Action only when `Click` is advertised, one action named "click"; Value when `numeric_value` is set (`Slider`, `Meter`, determinate `ProgressIndicator`, `SpinButton`); Selection on container roles (`TabList`, `RadioGroup`, `Grid`, `ListBox`, `Menu`, `MenuBar`, `ComboBox`); Image on `Role::Image` (`Avatar`); Hyperlink only for `Link` with a url (the kit's `Link` sets none: `grep -rn set_url crates` → 0). Text and EditableText never (no text runs). Document no.
  - Properties: name (`a11y_alt`; value text for role `Label`), description (hint), role, bounds, states Enabled, Sensitive, Focusable, Focused, Visible, Showing, Selected, Selectable, Checked, Checkable, Pressed, Indeterminate, Editable, Active, Modal, Busy, Required, Multiselectable, Horizontal, Vertical, ReadOnly, SingleLine, MultiLine; attributes placeholder-text, posinset, setsize, id, braillelabel, brailleroledescription; live regions (`Live` sends an AT-SPI Announcement when the name of a live node changes, `node.rs:631-633`; the kit sets none: `grep -rniE 'set_live' crates` → 0). Relations: `ControllerFor` only.
  - Roles the kit sets today (grep `a11y_role` in `crates/bk/src`): Button, Switch, CheckBox, RadioGroup, RadioButton, Slider, Meter, ProgressIndicator, SpinButton, PasswordInput, MultilineTextInput, DateInput, Group, Toolbar, Grid, GridCell, Row, Table, ColumnHeader, Tree, TreeItem, TabList, Tab, ListItem, Navigation, Splitter, MenuItem, Dialog, AlertDialog, Link, Image, Label (`Text`), Window (root, from Freya).
  - Not exported although the kit or AccessKit has them: `expanded`, `has_popup`, heading level, `labelled_by` and `described_by` relations, `author_id`, `custom_actions`, `Increment`/`Decrement`. These are limits of `accesskit_atspi_common` 0.21.1, not of the kit.
- **Linux counterparts of the Apple-only features**: Orca (screen reader; Braille through BRLTTY and the exported `braillelabel` attribute) over AT-SPI2 on the a11y bus (`org.a11y.Bus`). Rotors: Orca structural navigation by role; no custom rotors. Voice Control: none built in (third-party tools use accessible names). Switch Control: none in the default desktops (unverified). Zoom: the compositor's magnifier, so malaysia's job. Large Content Viewer: none. Orca and AT-SPI behavior was not run here: all findings are from reading sources.
- **WCAG 2.2 where the kit meets or misses**: 2.4.7 Focus Visible met (focus ring always drawn, `.claude/skills/barrierkit-app/styles.md` "Accessibility"; 3 px `FOCUS_RING`). 1.4.11 focus ring contrast enforced only for a custom accent under Increase Contrast (`ctappearance/src/resolve.rs:641-649`); named accents keep macOS's values. 1.4.3 on-accent text 4.5:1 enforced for custom accents only (`resolve.rs:795`). 2.5.8 Target Size (24 px): regular push button is 24 high (met); mini 16 and small 20 miss (`macos-control-metrics.md:33`); the 12 pt traffic lights miss, as in macOS (exception unverified). 1.4.4 Resize Text: `TEXT_SIZES` reaches 2.0 but only `Text` and sidebar items scale. 1.4.1 Use of Color: Differentiate Without Color reaches traffic lights only. 1.3.1 and 4.1.2: partial, see rows 1 to 6.
- **Unverified**: the label overwrite and the slider step-name case (`controls/slider.rs:229-236` sets `value` when a step has a name, which `create_node` copies into the label) are from code reading; the kit test `a_slider_announces_its_name_value_and_range` reads the raw builder, before `create_node`, so it does not see them. No Orca run. GSettings key names, `org.a11y.Status` `ScreenReaderEnabled`, `xdg-output-unstable-v1` in the pinned crates, the value of `horizontalSizeClass` on macOS and whether macOS 27 has Bold Text are from memory and not checked. Deviation from the brief's blocker vocabulary: limits of `accesskit_atspi_common` are labelled `Freya` because Freya pins it through `accesskit_unix`.

## Limits of this study

- No probe ran and no macOS behavior was measured. Rows that depend on a look
  or a timing say `measurement`.
- The closed list reads declarations, not behavior. A modifier the interface
  lists may do nothing on macOS (the reviews mark those they suspect).
- Absence claims rest on `grep` over `crates/`, the glossary and `agents/`. A
  feature written under an unusual name can escape; the rows quote the
  patterns.
- Freya, Skia and Wayland claims come from reading the pinned sources, not from
  running the kit. Whether the compositor (malaysia) serves a protocol is
  mostly unverified: `~/src/malaysia` was only read.
- Nothing was run on Orca, AT-SPI, KDE or GNOME.
